Result: 在内部和外部队列上的实验表明，UPolarSQ改善了OD/PPA分割性能，并支持可靠的极坐标原生生物标志物估计，适用于近视分析。

Conclusion: 极坐标域框架能够统一分割与量化过程，在共享几何表示下有效提升近视眼底OD/PPA分析的可信度与一致性。

Abstract: Myopia-induced posterior-pole remodeling is frequently accompanied by Optic Disc (OD) deformation and Peripapillary Atrophy (PPA), both of which provide clinically relevant structural biomarkers. In Cartesian fundus images, however, PPA often appears as an irregular and partially visible crescent adjacent to the OD, leading to fragmented segmentation and post-processing-dependent quantification. We propose UPolarSQ, a unified polar-domain framework for OD/PPA segmentation and biomarker quantification in myopic fundus images. UPolarSQ first maps an OD-centered region of interest into polar coordinates, where OD and PPA boundaries can be represented as radial profiles. It then employs UPolarSeg, a U-Net-based segmentation network enhanced with a Radial-Angular-Decoupled Module and boundary-aware auxiliary supervision to model anisotropic polar features and radial boundary transitions. Clinical biomarkers, including disc shape and PPA-width-related measurements, are deterministically extracted from the predicted polar masks, aligning segmentation and quantification within a shared geometric representation. Experiments on internal and external cohorts demonstrate that UPolarSQ improves OD/PPA segmentation and supports reliable polar-native biomarker estimation for myopic analysis.

</details>


### [138] [LASA: Language-and-Source-Anchored Alignment for Domain Generalized Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08805)
*Jinhong Zhu,Weiqi Yan,Shengchuan Zhang,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LASA框架解决域泛化语义分割中风格随机化破坏特征流形、特征归一化抑制判别性细节的问题，通过TSGST、DAQA和DADO三个协同模块实现文本与源域锚定的对齐，在多个基准上显著超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DGSS方法（风格随机化和特征归一化）虽能缓解域偏移，但分别存在粗粒度导致特征流形失真、刚性设计抑制判别性域敏感语义细节的缺陷。

Method: 提出LASA框架，包含三个组件：(1) TSGST利用源域特征作为结构锚点、VLM先验作为细粒度指导实现风格迁移，避免流形失真；(2) DAQA通过类别指导和域感知签名对目标查询进行重校准，恢复被抑制的判别性细节；(3) DADO将查询分布与共享分类器对齐，确保跨域类别响应一致。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的基准数据集上进行的大量实验表明，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: LASA框架通过源域结构锚定和语言模型细粒度引导的协同设计，有效解决了传统DGSS方法中特征完整性与域泛化能力之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Domain Generalization Semantic Segmentation (DGSS) focuses on generalizing knowledge from labeled source domains to unseen target domains where data is unavailable during the training phase. While conventional methods utilize style randomization or feature normalization to mitigate domain shifts, they often impair feature integrity. Specifically, style randomization distorts the underlying feature manifold due to its coarse-grained nature, while feature normalization suppresses discriminative, domain-sensitive semantic details owing to its rigid design. To address these limitations, we propose the Language-and-Source-Anchored Alignment (LASA) framework, which comprises three synergistic components: Text-and-Source-Guided Style Transfer (TSGST), Domain-Aware Query Adapter (DAQA), and Domain-Aware Decoder Optimizer (DADO). Concretely, the TSGST module addresses manifold distortion by utilizing source features as structural anchors and vision-language model (VLM) priors as fine-grained guidance. To restore suppressed discriminative and domain-sensitive details, the DAQA module recalibrates object queries via categorical guidance and domain-aware signatures, while the DADO module aligns the resulting query distributions with a shared classifier to ensure consistent categorical responses across domains. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [139] [AdapterMoE: A Two-Stage Hard-Routing Mixture-of-Experts Architecture for Multi-Crop Disease Recognition with Calibrated Rejection and Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08808)
*Pin-Hsun Huang,Shaou-Gang Miaou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AdapterMoE，一种基于确定性硬路由的MoE架构，用于多作物疾病识别。通过RouterHead进行分类与OOD拒绝、五个每作物Adapter配合冻结的EfficientNet-B0骨干实现疾病判别，显著降低训练成本（约9%），支持按作物增量扩展，且在PlantVillage上达到与最佳基线统计上无显著差异的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软路由MoE在作物疾病识别中面临专家坍缩（少数专家主导、无作物语义对应）、重训成本高、对非目标输入的拒绝不稳定以及精度饱和等问题。作者将目标从单纯追求精度转向部署成本、扩展灵活性和拒绝稳定性之间的权衡。

Method: 确定性硬路由方案：RouterHead负责作物分类并通过最大softmax概率阈值拒绝非目标作物；双重门的Energy+KNN分布外检测模块捕获分布偏移输入；冻结EfficientNet-B0骨干上部署五个每作物Adapter（经温度缩放校准）进行疾病判别。专家在数据层面硬隔离，避免专家坍缩，并提供add_crop接口支持局部按作物增量更新。

Result: 在PlantVillage数据集（5种作物、26类）上，经过公平的五系统对比，AdapterMoE的Macro-F1与最佳基线统计上无显著差异（在0.24个百分点范围内），同时训练成本降至全网络基线的约9%。

Conclusion: AdapterMoE通过硬路由设计在保持与最佳基线相当的识别精度的同时，大幅降低训练成本并实现灵活的按作物增量扩展，在精度、成本与拒绝稳定性之间取得了更优的平衡。

Abstract: Timely crop-disease identification is critical to food security. Multi-crop recognition suits Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), but conventional soft-routing MoE learns crop assignment freely end-to-end, letting a few experts dominate (expert collapse) with no semantic correspondence to crops, and facing high retraining costs, unstable rejection of non-target inputs, and a saturated accuracy ceiling. We shift the objective from accuracy toward a trade-off among deployment cost, scaling flexibility, and rejection stability, using deterministic hard routing. We propose AdapterMoE: a RouterHead classifies the crop and rejects non-target crops via a Maximum Softmax Probability threshold, with a dual-gate Energy+KNN out-of-distribution module catching distribution-shifted inputs; five per-crop Adapters atop a frozen EfficientNet-B0 backbone discriminate diseases, each calibrated via Temperature Scaling. Because experts are hard-isolated at the data level, the design avoids expert collapse and exposes an add_crop interface for local, per-crop updates instead of full retraining. On PlantVillage (5 crops, 26 classes), across a fair five-system comparison, AdapterMoE attains accuracy statistically indistinguishable from the best baselines (Macro-F1 within a 0.24-point band) while cutting training cost to about 9% of full-network baselines, expanding to a new crop in

</details>


### [140] [360CityArena: A Realistic Virtual Urban Navigation Benchmark for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08814)
*Kenta Watanabe,Atsuyuki Miyai,Mizuki Takenawa,Kiyoharu Aizawa,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了360CityArena基准，用于评估具身代理在基于360度视频构建的逼真城市环境中的探索能力。该基准基于东京秋叶原地区的602段360度视频重建，包含175个手工设计的任务，涵盖环境理解、路径推理和空间推理三类。评估显示最强模型Gemini 2.5 Flash仅达17.1%的准确率，远低于人类的77.3%，揭示了城市级具身导航与推理仍面临巨大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有户外基准要么缺乏足够的照片级真实感，要么复杂度不足，与真实城市环境存在较大差距，无法有效评估具身代理的城市探索能力。

Method: 基于东京秋叶原地区602段360度视频（覆盖85条街道）构建逼真城市环境，设计175个手工任务，涵盖环境理解、路径推理和空间推理三大类别，并使用基于LMM的先进代理进行系统评估。

Result: 最强模型Gemini 2.5 Flash在城市探索任务中仅达到17.1%的准确率，而人类水平为77.3%，表明现有LMM代理在城市级具身导航和推理方面远未达到人类能力。

Conclusion: 360CityArena为照片级真实城市区域导航和空间推理提供了一个必要且具有挑战性的测试平台，揭示了城市规模具身导航与推理中仍存在的重大挑战。

Abstract: We present 360CityArena, a benchmark for evaluating the urban exploration capabilities of embodied agents within a photorealistic environment constructed from 360-degree videos. Existing outdoor benchmarks either lack sufficient photorealism or complexity, resulting in a considerable gap from real-world urban environments. 360CityArena is built on a realistic reconstruction of the Akihabara district in Tokyo, Japan, using 602 360-degree video segments covering 85 streets, and consists of 175 meticulously human-crafted tasks. It encompasses three task categories: Environment Understanding, Path Reasoning, and Spatial Reasoning, covering fundamental abilities required for urban exploration, such as localization, landmark search, path planning, and relational spatial reasoning, thereby enabling comprehensive evaluation in realistic urban scenes. Our evaluation using state-of-the-art LMM-based agents shows that even the strongest model, Gemini 2.5 Flash, performs far below human level (human: 77.3% vs. Gemini 2.5 Flash: 17.1%), revealing substantial challenges that remain in city-scale embodied navigation and reasoning. 360CityArena provides a necessary and challenging testbed for photorealistic urban-district navigation and spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [141] [MRI super-resolution in ten sampling steps using a diffusion bridge model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08819)
*Mojtaba Safari,Hang Yu,Zach Eidex,Mingzhe Hu,Ryan J. Sanford,Alexandru Florea,Shansong Wang,Chih-Wei Chang,Erik H Middlebrooks,Aditya Juloori,Stanley L. Liauw,Ralph Weichselbaum,Xiaofeng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SR-DBM，一种基于扩散桥模型的超分辨率方法，用于重建高分辨率MRI图像。该方法通过Doob's h-变换将超分辨率建模为低分辨率与高分辨率图像分布之间的随机传输，从测量到的解剖结构而非高斯噪声初始化，仅需10个采样步骤即可重建高质量图像。在两个MRI数据集上优于九种对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI虽具有优异的软组织对比度，但长时间采集导致患者不适和运动伪影，存在空间分辨率与扫描时间之间的权衡。现有基于扩散的超分辨率方法需要大量采样步骤，且从高斯先验初始化，不适合图像恢复任务。

Method: 提出SR-DBM（超分辨率扩散桥模型），将超分辨率建模为LR与HR图像分布之间的随机传输。通过均值回归随机微分方程的Doob's h-变换，将过程锚定在配对的HR和LR图像端点上，从测量的解剖信息而非高斯噪声初始化。网络在每个仅10步的采样步骤中预测干净图像，通过确定性反向轨迹重建HR图像。

Result: 在7T脑部T1 MP2RAGE和盆腔T2加权前列腺图像数据集上，SR-DBM在两个数据集上均取得最高PSNR和SSIM及最低GMSD（脑部：27.66±1.52 dB, 0.96±0.02, 7.96±1.86；前列腺：27.87±2.29 dB, 0.80±0.05, 8.38±1.44），与所有对比方法相比均有统计学显著提升（双侧Wilcoxon符号秩检验，Holm校正，p<0.05）。最强的基线SR-EMamba排名第二。定性上，SR-DBM产生的残差误差最小，最好地保留了精细结构和病灶。

Conclusion: SR-DBM通过锚定扩散桥端点并从测量解剖结构初始化，实现了高效且高质量的MRI超分辨率重建，仅需10步采样即可超越现有方法，在定量指标和定性图像质量上均表现最佳。

Abstract: Objective. MRI provides excellent soft-tissue contrast, but long acquisition times can cause patient discomfort and lead to motion artifacts, forcing a trade-off between spatial resolution and scan time. Diffusion-based super-resolution (SR) reconstructs high-resolution (HR) images from low-resolution (LR) inputs, but typically needs many sampling steps and initializes from a Gaussian prior ill-suited to image restoration. We developed an efficient diffusion framework that reconstructs HR MRI directly from LR data. Approach. We propose super-resolution diffusion bridge model (SR-DBM), a super-resolution diffusion bridge model that casts SR as a stochastic transport between the LR and HR image distributions. Through a Doob's h-transform of a mean-reverting stochastic differential equation, SR-DBM pins the process to the paired HR and LR images at its endpoints, initializing reconstruction from the measured anatomy rather than from Gaussian noise. The HR image is recovered by a deterministic reverse trajectory in which a network predicts the clean image at each of only ten sampling steps. We evaluated SR-DBM on ultra-high-field 7T brain T1 MP2RAGE maps and pelvic T2-weighted prostate images against nine comparison methods using PSNR, SSIM, GMSD, and LPIPS. Main results. SR-DBM attained the highest PSNR and SSIM and the lowest GMSD on both datasets (brain: 27.66+-1.52 dB, 0.96+-0.02, 7.96+-1.86$; prostate: 27.87+-2.29 dB, 0.80+-0.05, 8.38+- 1.44), with statistically significant gains over every comparison method (two-sided Wilcoxon signed-rank test with Holm correction, p<0.05). The strongest baseline, SR-EMamba, ranked second. Qualitatively, SR-DBM produced the smallest residual errors and best preserved fine structures and lesions.

</details>


### [142] [LogiShot: Logically Coherent Cross-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08820)
*Shuai Guo,Yuhang Yang,Zeyu Zhang,Pengfei Yu,Wei Zhai,Yang Cao,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LogiShot，一种用于跨镜头视频生成的方法，通过联合编码上下文视频与条件信号获取密集多模态线索，并在生成过程中维护视觉记忆，从而实现跨镜头的逻辑连贯性和视觉一致性。作者还构建了包含11万个样本的数据集和专用基准来评估跨镜头逻辑连贯性，实验表明LogiShot在多个镜头上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前大多数跨镜头视频生成流程依赖孤立的文本脚本或显式参考图像来指定生成内容。当用户指令不明确或模糊时，生成的片段虽然视觉上看似合理，但无法与整体叙事对齐，导致内容脱节。因此需要建立跨镜头的逻辑连接和视觉一致性。

Method: LogiShot通过两条互补路径整合信息：1）联合编码上下文视频和其他条件信号，生成密集多模态线索，为跨镜头生成提供视觉-语义证据；2）在整个生成过程中维护上下文视频的视觉记忆，以保持跨镜头的视觉一致性。此外构建了110K样本的数据集和专用评估基准。

Result: 实验表明LogiShot在跨多个镜头的逻辑连贯性方面持续优于现有基线模型。

Conclusion: LogiShot通过多模态线索编码和视觉记忆机制有效实现了跨镜头视频生成的逻辑连贯性和视觉一致性，模型和数据将公开发布以促进该领域研究。

Abstract: Generating cross-shot videos that are logically connected is essential for content creation. Currently, most cross-shot video-generation workflows, such as short-drama production, still rely on isolated textual scripts or explicit reference images to specify the generated content. Consequently, when user instructions are underspecified or ambiguous, a generated clip may appear visually plausible on its own but fail to align with the overall narrative, leading to disjointed content. We argue that achieving cross-shot logical coherence in video generation requires establishing logical connections across shots and maintaining visual consistency. To this end, we propose LogiShot, which incorporates information through two complementary paths: 1) LogiShot jointly encodes the context video and other conditioning signals, yielding dense multimodal cues that provide visual-semantic evidence for cross-shot generation; 2) the model maintains a visual memory of the context video throughout generation to preserve visual consistency across shots. Additionally, we construct a dataset with 110K samples and a dedicated benchmark for evaluating cross-shot logical coherence. Experiments demonstrate that LogiShot consistently outperforms existing baselines in terms of logical coherence across multiple shots. Model and data will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [143] [Visual Token Codec: Unleashing Spatial Redundancy for ViT Feature Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08832)
*Donghui Feng,Fengxi Zhang,Changsheng Gao,Wenhan Yang,Qi Wang,Qunshan Gu,Hongwei Hu,Zhengxue Cheng,Li Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VTC（Visual Token Codec），一种双路径学习的视觉Token编解码器，将全局Token和补丁Token分离编码，利用补丁Token在原始网格上的二维空间相关性，通过空间-通道上下文熵模型实现高效特征压缩。在DINOv2和SAM3上，VTC在90%未压缩特征性能下将码率降低15.7倍到37.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉基础模型分布式部署时，在计算节点间传输中间Token特征需要高效压缩。现有ViT特征编解码器将异构的全局Token和补丁Token展平为L×C伪图像，熵模型主要捕获序列轴依赖关系，忽略了补丁Token原生二维网格结构所蕴含的空间相关性，导致压缩效率受限。

Method: VTC采用双路径编解码框架：全局Token使用轻量级因子化先验压缩，补丁Token在补丁网格上使用空间-通道上下文熵模型编码。此外，引入后续ViT块的特征匹配监督以支持中间层压缩，并在单一编解码器内集成可变码率模块，实现实际部署中的码率自适应。

Result: 在DINOv2和SAM3上的分类、分割和检测任务中，VTC一致优于代表性ViT特征编码基线。在达到未压缩特征90%性能时，VTC将码率降低15.7倍至37.4倍。论文还提供了中间层率失真分析，以支持传输和存储场景的部署。

Conclusion: VTC通过显式建模补丁Token的二维空间结构，显著提升了ViT中间特征的压缩效率，为分布式视觉大模型的部署提供了实用的特征通信解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed deployment of large vision foundation models often partitions a ViT backbone and exchanges intermediate token features between computing nodes, making efficient feature compression critical under bandwidth and computation constraints. Existing ViT feature codecs typically flatten heterogeneous global and patch tokens into an L x C pseudo image, causing entropy models to mainly capture sequence-axis dependencies while overlooking the native two-dimensional patch-grid structure. In this paper, we show that ViT patch tokens retain strong local spatial correlations on the original grid. To exploit this structural prior, we propose the Visual Token Codec (VTC), a dual-path learned codec that separates global and patch tokens into dedicated coding paths. Global tokens are compressed with a lightweight factorized prior, whereas patch tokens are encoded on the patch-token grid using a spatial-channel context entropy model. To support intermediate-layer compression and practical rate adaptation, VTC further incorporates feature-matching supervision after subsequent ViT blocks and variable-rate modules within a single codec. Experiments on DINOv2 and SAM3 show that VTC consistently outperforms representative ViT feature coding baselines on classification, segmentation, and detection tasks. At 90% of uncompressed-feature performance, VTC reduces bitrate by 15.7x-37.4x across these tasks. We further provide intermediate-layer rate-utility analyses for practical transmission- and storage-oriented deployment scenarios.

</details>


### [144] [SLAP: Selective Local Vision-Language Alignment for Fish Re-Identification via Partial Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08840)
*Cigdem Beyan,Tonje Knutsen Sordalen,Kim Tallaksen Halvorsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于选择性局部视觉-语言对齐框架的鱼类个体重识别方法，通过部分最优传输（POT）在视觉补丁嵌入和多个身份感知提示嵌入之间建立局部对应关系，强调最强的跨模态对应并避免弱匹配区域的强制对齐，从而获得更具判别性的视觉表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CLIP-based鱼类重识别方法过度依赖全局图像-文本对齐，使得背景和弱判别性区域也会参与跨模态监督，而个体重识别中的判别性线索通常集中在特定身体区域而非均匀分布，因此需要更精细的局部对齐策略。

Method: 提出选择性局部视觉-语言对齐框架，利用部分最优传输（POT）在视觉补丁嵌入与多个身份感知提示嵌入之间建立选择性匹配，而非强制穷举对应关系。框架端到端训练，推理时仅保留适配后的视觉编码器。

Result: 在纵向Symphodus melops数据集上的实验表明，该方法在闭集和开集评估协议下均持续优于现有的CLIP-based重识别方法；在其他数据集上的额外评估进一步证明了该方法在多种海洋重识别基准上的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过选择性局部视觉-语言对齐，该方法有效地避免了弱匹配区域的干扰，显著提升了个体鱼类重识别的判别性和泛化性能。

Abstract: Individual fish re-identification (ReID) is a fine-grained recognition problem in which identity-discriminative cues are often localized to specific body regions rather than distributed uniformly across the animal. Nevertheless, recent CLIP-based ReID methods rely predominantly on global image-text alignment, allowing background and weakly discriminative regions to contribute to cross-modal supervision. We propose a selective local vision-language alignment framework that establishes localized correspondences between visual patch embeddings and multiple identity-aware prompt embeddings through Partial Optimal Transport (POT). Rather than enforcing exhaustive correspondence, POT enables selective matching between visual patches and prompt embeddings, allowing the model to emphasize the strongest cross-modal correspondences while avoiding forced alignment of weakly matching regions, thereby yielding more discriminative visual representations for retrieval. The framework is trained end-to-end, while only the adapted visual encoder is retained during inference. Experiments on the longitudinal Symphodus melops dataset demonstrate consistent improvements over recent CLIP-based ReID methods under both closed-set and open-set evaluation protocols. Additional evaluations on other datasets further demonstrate the generalization capability of the proposed method across diverse marine ReID benchmarks.

</details>


### [145] [Toward Mask Annotation-Free Surgical Instrument Segmentation from Endoscopic Images Using Text-Prompted Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM3)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08844)
*Nakul Poudel,Richard Simon,Cristian A. Linte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段框架，利用SAM3的零样本文本提示能力（使用通用词"tool"生成二元掩码）并结合微调的Qwen视觉语言模型进行器械分类，实现无需像素级标注或人工交互的手术器械实例分割，在EndoVis 2017和2018数据集上验证了其有效性，虽不及全监督方法但显著优于直接使用SAM3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术器械分割方法依赖像素级标注或手动空间提示，限制了可扩展性和自动化程度。虽然SAM3支持文本提示的自动分割，但器械名称作为文本提示因领域差距无法直接使用，亟需一种免标注、免交互的实例级分割方案。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：第一阶段利用自然语言对齐的通用提示词"tool"调用SAM3的零样本能力生成二元掩码；第二阶段将掩码区域输入微调后的Qwen视觉语言模型进行器械分类，将二元掩码扩展为实例级分割结果。

Result: 在EndoVis 2017和2018数据集上，两阶段方法虽未达到全监督方法的性能，但显著优于直接使用SAM3文本提示进行实例级器械分割的效果。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了SAM3在手术器械分割中的局限性与潜力，为走向无标注的手术器械分割提供了一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Surgical instrument segmentation is a fundamental task for computer-assisted interventions, yet most existing methods rely on pixel-level annotations or manual spatial prompts, which limit scalability and automation. The recently introduced Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM3) offers a pathway to annotation-free, automatic segmentation via text-based prompting; however, the instrument name as a text prompt could not be directly used due to a large domain gap. To overcome these limitations, we propose a two-stage framework that achieves instance-level segmentation without requiring ground truth masks or manual interaction. In the first stage, we leverage a natural-language-aligned generic prompt - "tool" - to produce binary masks using SAM3's zero-shot capability. In the second stage, these masks are extended to instance-level by integrating a vision-language model (Qwen) that is fine-tuned on SAM3-generated masked regions for instrument classification. We evaluate our approach on the EndoVis 2017 and 2018 datasets. Results show that, while our two-stage approach does not reach the performance of current fully supervised methods, it significantly outperforms the direct use of SAM3 for instance-level instrument segmentation with text prompts. Overall, our findings highlight both the limitations and potential of SAM3, suggesting a promising direction toward annotation-free surgical instrument segmentation.

</details>


### [146] [Zero-Shot Traffic Accident Detection via a Coarse-to-Fine VLM-Tracking Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08867)
*Dipit Saha,Shah Mohammad Abdul Mannan,Mohammad Raihan Rashid,Ruwad Naswan,Ahnaf Tahmid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无需训练的两阶段粗到细流程，结合冻结的Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct视觉语言模型、YOLO11x目标检测和BoT-SORT跟踪，在无标注真实训练数据的条件下，实现交通监控视频中碰撞事件的时空定位与类型分类，在官方2,027段真实CCTV视频测试集上取得0.504的三方位调和平均分，超越所有组织者发布基线22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通监控摄像头持续记录事故，但将原始CCTV视频转换为结构化事件记录（明确碰撞发生的时间、地点和类型）在大规模场景下尚未解决。ACCIDENT @ CVPR基准测试在严格约束下评估此联合预测任务：不可使用标注的真实世界训练数据。

Method: 提出一种免训练的两遍粗到细流水线：第一遍稀疏采样完整视频片段以锚定碰撞时刻；第二遍在估计时刻附近的紧密时间窗口内，使用带有稳定车辆身份和归一化边界框坐标标注的帧进行重新审视，为模型同时提供视觉叠加和场景的显式数值描述。该流程结合冻结的Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct视觉语言模型、YOLO11x目标检测和BoT-SORT跟踪。

Result: 在官方2,027段真实CCTV测试集上，系统实现三方位调和平均分0.504，超越所有组织者发布的基线，包括最佳多模型集成（0.412），相对提升22%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，无需训练数据，仅通过精心设计的推理流程和现成视觉语言模型，即可在真实交通监控视频中有效完成碰撞事件的联合时空定位与分类，显著超越已有的监督/集成基线方法。

Abstract: Traffic surveillance cameras capture accidents continuously, yet converting raw CCTV footage into structured event records that pinpoint when, where, and what type of collision occurred remains unsolved at scale. The ACCIDENT @ CVPR benchmark evaluates exactly this joint prediction under a strict constraint: no labeled real-world training data is available. We introduce a training-free, two-pass coarse-to-fine pipeline that pairs a frozen Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct vision-language model with YOLO11x object detection and BoT-SORT tracking. A first pass sparsely samples the full clip to anchor the collision moment in time; a second pass re-examines a tight window around that estimate using frames annotated with stable vehicle identities and normalized bounding-box coordinates, which gives the model both a visual overlay and an explicit numeric description of the same scene. On the official 2,027-clip real-CCTV test set, our system achieves a three-way harmonic mean score of 0.504, surpassing all organizer-published baselines including the best multi-model ensemble (0.412) by a 22% relative margin.

</details>


### [147] [Sparse Attention to Emotion: Efficient Facial Emotion Recognition via Token Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08873)
*Aya Manel Zitouni,Aicha Zenakhri,Karim Haroun,Larbi Boubchir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为SAE（Sparse Attention to Emotion）的轻量级面部表情识别模型，基于"并非所有面部信息都是表情识别所必需"的假设，通过丢弃无关图像令牌来大幅降低计算复杂度。实验表明，即使在抑制90%的图像令牌后，SAE仍能在RAF-DB数据集上达到最先进水平，同时将计算复杂度降低多达90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Vision Transformer的面部表情识别方法具有O(N²)的二次复杂度，难以在边缘设备上部署。作者假设面部表情识别并不需要全部面部信息，眼睛、嘴巴和部分脸颊等关键区域已足够用于情绪识别，因此希望通过稀疏注意力机制来降低计算成本。

Method: 提出SAE模型，通过稀疏注意力机制识别并丢弃对情绪上下文没有增益的图像令牌，仅保留眼睛、嘴巴和脸颊等具有判别性信息的区域令牌进行注意力计算，从而在保持准确率的同时显著降低计算复杂度。

Result: SAE在RAF-DB数据集上取得了新的最先进结果，并且在抑制90%图像令牌的情况下仍能保持与现有方法相当的准确率，计算复杂度降低了高达90%。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了面部表情识别中信息冗余的假设，证明通过稀疏化处理可以在大幅降低计算成本的同时保持甚至提升识别性能，为边缘设备上的轻量级面部表情识别提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Facial Emotion Recognition (FER) is an important task that has significant implications across various fields such as biometrics, health, and human-computer interaction. Current Vision Transformer-based approaches display quadratic complexity $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$, with N being the input sequence length, making them cumbersome to deploy at the edge. In this paper, we hypothesize that the FER task does not necessarily require all facial information to correctly interpret emotional states, as specific regions such as the eyes, the mouth, and parts of the cheeks carry discriminative information that can be sufficient to recognize emotions. Based on this, we propose Sparse Attention to Emotion (SAE), a model that discards image tokens that have no added value to the emotional context, while preserving good accuracy and achieving a significant gain in computational cost. Surprisingly, even after suppressing 90\% of the image tokens, our model achieves competitive accuracy to state of the art methods at much lower cost, providing a lightweight Facial Emotion Recognition approach. Experimental results demonstrate that SAE achieves new state of the art results on the RAF-DB dataset while reducing the computational complexity by up to 90\%.

</details>


### [148] [AeroReformer2: Spoken-Query Referring Segmentation for Aerial Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08874)
*Rui Li,Chenxi Duan,Haoyang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于语音查询的遥感图像指代分割基准数据集（由RISBench扩展而来，加入口音和声音多样化的语音），并提出了一个高效的双边网络模型，在干净测试集上达到62.09%的mIoU，优于最强基线5.38个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口语提供了一种自然、免提的方式来指定遥感图像中的任意目标，但现有的遥感指代图像分割基准只接受书面表达式，存在语音查询与视觉分割之间的空白。

Method: 提出了一个由RISBench衍生的语音查询基准数据集，包含口音和声音多样化的语音，以及转子、风和混合干扰等噪声条件下的难例测试集。同时提出高效双边网络：结合边界保持视觉路径与令牌保留语音编码、核线性交叉模态注意力和分辨率精化头，在两种尺度上条件化视觉特征而无需物化稠密的语音-视觉亲和矩阵，并使用高分辨率视觉特征恢复精细边界。

Result: 在干净测试集上达到62.09%的mIoU和68.22%的oIoU，分别超过最强音频适配遥感基线5.38和2.08个百分点；在困难测试集上也保持了54.09%的最佳mIoU。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对遥感图像全句口语查询指代分割的基准和模型研究，代码将公开提供。

Abstract: Spoken language offers a natural, hands-free interface for specifying an arbitrary target in dense remote-sensing imagery, yet existing referring remote-sensing image segmentation benchmarks accept only written expressions. To bridge this gap, we introduce \dataset, a spoken-query benchmark derived from RISBench that adds accent- and voice-diverse speech while preserving the original image, mask, and data splits. Its hard evaluation sets combine rotor, wind, and mixed interference with three signal-to-noise levels. We also propose \model, an efficient bilateral network that combines a boundary-preserving visual path with token-preserving speech encoding, kernel linear cross-modal attention, and a resolution refinement head. The design conditions visual features at two scales without materializing a dense speech--visual affinity matrix, then restores fine boundaries using high-resolution visual features. On the clean test split, \model with Swin-Base achieves 62.09\% mean intersection over union (mIoU) and 68.22\% overall intersection over union (oIoU), outperforming the strongest audio-adapted remote-sensing baseline by 5.38 and 2.08 percentage points, respectively. It retains the best hard-set mIoU at 54.09\%. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first benchmark and model study of full-sentence spoken-query referring segmentation for remote-sensing imagery. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [149] [City Sentinel: A Unified AI-Based Smart Surveillance Framework for Real-Time Multi-Threat Detection Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08887)
*Hanan Syed Shabir,Noor Fatima,Safia Baloch,Masroor Hussain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出City Sentinel，一个统一的AI监控框架，将人脸识别、车牌识别、火焰烟雾检测、武器检测、暴力检测和交通事故检测六种能力集成到一个可扩展平台中，在RTX 3060 GPU上实现743ms中位延迟，支持4路并发RTSP流，面匹配率91.2%，车牌识别准确率85.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速城市化增加了对多公共安全风险同时监控的需求，但传统系统使用分散的解决方案分别处理人脸识别、车辆识别、火灾检测和行为分析，导致基础设施碎片化、操作员需管理多个接口。

Method: 采用模块化多模型架构：Next.js操作员仪表板、FastAPI后端、云PostgreSQL事件存储、InsightFace和YOLOv8视觉模型、EasyOCR车牌识别；摄像头流通过专用推理worker使用RTSP处理。

Result: 在RTX 3060上实现中位端到端延迟743ms，支持4路并发RTSP流（在2秒延迟限制内）；面匹配率91.2%，车牌读取准确率85.7%，火焰/刀具/武器检测模块mAP@0.5为0.846-0.889；用户验收测试中身份注册<1分钟，实时识别约12秒。

Conclusion: 模块化、开源、多模型架构能够提供广泛的监控覆盖、云审计能力和添加新检测功能的灵活性，同时保持实用的实时性能。

Abstract: Rapid urbanization has increased the need for surveillance systems that can monitor multiple public safety risks at the same time. Traditional systems often use separate solutions for facial recognition, vehicle identification, fire detection, and behavioral analysis, resulting in fragmented infrastructure and multiple interfaces for operators to manage. This paper presents City Sentinel, a unified AI-based surveillance framework that integrates six detection capabilities into one scalable platform: facial recognition, automatic number plate recognition (ANPR), fire and smoke detection, weapon and knife detection, violence detection, and road accident detection. The system combines a Next.js operator dashboard, FastAPI backend, cloud-based PostgreSQL event storage, InsightFace and YOLOv8 vision models, and EasyOCR for plate recognition. Camera streams are processed through dedicated inference workers using RTSP. On a workstation equipped with an NVIDIA RTX 3060 GPU, the system achieves a median end-to-end latency of 743 ms and supports four concurrent RTSP streams within a two-second latency limit. It achieves a 91.2% face-match rate, 85.7% plate-reading accuracy, and mAP@0.5 scores of 0.846 to 0.889 across the fire, knife, and weapon detection modules. In user-acceptance testing, operators could enroll a new identity in under one minute and identify a flagged person from live footage in an average of 12 seconds. The results demonstrate that a modular, open-source, multi-model architecture can provide broad surveillance coverage, cloud-based auditability, and flexibility for adding new detection capabilities while maintaining practical real-time performance.

</details>


### [150] [From Recovery to Drop-off: How Action Post-training Reduces a VLM's Late-Layer Depth Decodability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08904)
*Alexander Hackett,Arnaud Denis-Remillard,Axel Cassou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探究了视觉语言模型（VLM）在经过动作后训练变成视觉语言动作模型（VLA）后，其空间理解能力（特别是深度感知）的退化情况。研究发现VLA在每个解码器层的深度解码能力都更差（称为"地板"效应），且在其最终层出现额外的退化坍塌（称为"悬崖"效应）。因果分析表明"悬崖"效应源于晚期层MLP写入的干扰，而非注意力机制。模块级分解进一步证明基础VLM的深度信息主要累积在MLP写入中，而动作后训练破坏了这一能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解VLM在转变为VLA的过程中损失了多少空间理解能力，特别是深度感知这一基础空间几何能力，并定位退化发生的具体机制和层位置，为改进VLA训练提供指导。

Method: 使用权重匹配的开源基础VLM/VLA对（Molmo2-ER和MolmoAct2-LIBERO），从每个解码器层探测深度感知能力。通过消融实验（移除晚期层MLP写入或注意力）进行因果定位，并进行模块级分解分析。

Result: 发现两个退化模式：各层一致的"地板"退化，以及晚期层的"悬崖"退化。因果定位显示晚期层MLP写入干扰是"悬崖"效应的主因，而注意力消融和基础VLM中的相同干预均无类似恢复效果。

Conclusion: 动作后训练通过干扰晚期层MLP写入中的累积深度信息，导致VLA的空间理解能力显著退化。这一发现为理解VLA训练中的灾难性遗忘提供了精细的机制级洞察。

Abstract: How much of a vision-language model's (VLM) spatial understanding remains after the action post-training process of building a vision-language-action model (VLA)? We probe depth perception, a primitive of spatiogeometric understanding, from every decoder layer of a weight-matched open-source base VLM/VLA pair: Molmo2-ER and MolmoAct2-LIBERO. First, the VLA decodes depth worse at every layer, a persistent gap we call the floor. Second, the degradation is not uniform: while the base VLM's depth decodability improves through its final layers, the VLA's collapses, an additional late-layer drop we call the cliff. We causally localize the cliff to late-layer MLP interference: ablating the late-layer MLP writes recovers the majority of the terminal decodability cliff, while matched attention ablations and the same intervention in the weight-matched base VLM produce no comparable recovery. A module-level decomposition explains this dissociation: the base VLM carries depth most accessibly in accumulated MLP writes, whereas action post-training collapses depth decodability in the late accumulated writes.

</details>


### [151] [ToolVision: Learning When and How to Use Visual Tools with Capability-Aligned Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08907)
*Delin Mao,Chenghao Sun,Jingwei Song,Chishui Chen,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolVision，一种解决多模态模型工具使用中SFT与RL两个阶段监督对齐偏差的训练方法。SFT阶段通过多智能体管道和委员会逐步评分来筛选高质量工具使用轨迹；RL阶段仅对工具带来明确收益的问题给予奖励。ToolVision-8B在七个基准上均超基础模型，并在多个高分辨率基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型通过代码调用视觉工具来补偿感知限制，但现有的SFT-then-RL训练流程存在两阶段监督错位：SFT阶段学生模型会模仿教师模型依赖强大感知能力的工具调用模式而无法真正学会使用工具；RL阶段纯粹的结果奖励要么抑制工具使用（因工具执行失败是负担），要么因全面奖励而鼓励无效但形式上正确的工具操作。

Method: ToolVision包含两个关键机制：(1) SFT阶段使用多智能体管道探索候选轨迹，由包含学生规模模型的委员会对每一步的证据增益进行评分以排序和剪枝搜索分支，仅保留成功执行且最终答案正确的轨迹用于SFT；(2) RL阶段先对比有无工具时的学习器性能，只对在工具能带来明确收益的问题上的成功工具使用给予奖励。两个信号均从公共任务数据自动构建，无需额外人工标注。

Result: ToolVision-8B在所有七个主要基准上优于其基础模型，在全部三个高分辨率基准上超越Thyme-7B、CodeVision-8B和CodeDance-7B，并在V*和HRBench 8K上超越Qwen3-VL-32B-Thinking。

Conclusion: 通过针对SFT和RL两个阶段分别设计对齐机制（SFT强调选好工具使用轨迹，RL强调只在工具真正有帮助时才奖励），ToolVision有效解决了多模态模型工具学习中的监督错位问题，且完全基于自动构建的信号，无需额外人工标注。

Abstract: Thinking with images allows a multimodal model to compensate for limited perception by invoking visual tools through code. Yet the prevailing SFT-then-RL recipe creates a different supervision misalignment at each stage. SFT is expected to teach how to use tools, but trajectories from stronger teachers may succeed through perceptual capabilities that a smaller student cannot reliably reproduce or exploit, causing the student to imitate tool-call patterns without learning how to make them useful. RL is expected to teach when to use tools, but outcome-only rewards make fallible tool execution a liability and suppress tool use, whereas a blanket bonus for every correct tool-using trajectory encourages valid but ineffective operations. To address these two misalignments, we introduce ToolVision. During SFT, a multi-agent pipeline explores candidate trajectories, and a committee including student-scale models scores stepwise evidence gain to rank and prune the search branches. Only successfully executed trajectories with correct final answers are retained for SFT. Before RL, ToolVision compares the learner's performance with and without tools, then rewards successful tool use only on questions where tools provide a clear benefit. Both signals are constructed automatically from public task data without additional human annotations of tool use or necessity. ToolVision-8B improves over its base on all seven main benchmarks, surpasses Thyme-7B, CodeVision-8B, and CodeDance-7B on all three high-resolution benchmarks, and outperforms Qwen3-VL-32B-Thinking on V* and HRBench 8K. We will publicly release the datasets and source code.

</details>


### [152] [From Noise to Meaning: Meaningful Secret Sharing with Tamper Detection for Facial Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08924)
*Ajnas Muhammed,Iurii Medvedev,Nuno Gonçalves*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新方法，将视觉秘密共享中类似噪声的秘密份额嵌入到具有感知透明性的封面图像中（使用自适应最低有效位隐写术），并结合强数字水印与密码学哈希实现双层认证，从而同时解决秘密份额显眼、易被针对收集的问题以及缺少篡改检测的完整性缺陷，并在多个公开人脸数据集上验证了更高的识别精度、隐私性、安全性和完整性保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于AI的人脸识别系统日益普及，训练所用的敏感生物特征数据需要保护。现有的视觉秘密共享方法虽然能将人脸图像拆分为随机分布的份额，但份额看起来像噪声，容易被识别为加密内容而遭受定向收集和"先收割后解密"攻击；此外，视觉秘密共享缺乏篡改检测机制，攻击者可修改份额威胁重建完整性。因此需要一种既能消除份额显眼性又能提供完整性验证的新方法。

Method: 提出一种新型框架：1) 使用视觉秘密共享将人脸图像拆分为秘密份额；2) 采用自适应最低有效位（LSB）隐写术，将秘密份额嵌入到具有感知透明性的封面图像中，使秘密数据看起来是正常视觉内容而非噪声；3) 结合强数字水印和密码学哈希实现两层认证机制，保护份额完整性，抵抗比特翻转、裁剪和替换攻击。

Result: 在多个公开人脸数据集上的大量实验表明，该技术不仅消除了秘密份额的显眼性并保证完整性，还在人脸识别（FR）精度上表现更优，同时对比特翻转、裁剪和替换攻击具有高抵抗力。

Conclusion: 该框架为保护人脸数据设立了新标准，将隐私、安全和完整性三者同时纳入保护范畴，在视觉秘密共享的基础上同时解决了份额显眼性问题与完整性验证缺失问题。

Abstract: Popularity of AI-based face recognition system directly demands protection of sensitive biometric data used for training. Visual secret sharing is an interesting idea, as it splits facial images into secret shares that look random and spread across many institutions. However, these shares look like noise and can easily spark suspicion and recognized as encrypted content. This makes them open to targeted collection and harvest-now-decrypt-later attacks. Additionally, visual secret sharing does not detect tampering, allowing attackers to modify shares and threaten the integrity of reconstruction. In this paper, we introduce a new method that turns distracting noise-like secret shares into visually appealing cover images with additional cryptographic tamper detection. The proposed technique works with visual secret sharing and introduces cover images to embed the shares using adaptive least significant bit steganography. Here, cover images with perceptual transparency are used to store secret shares while guaranteeing complete privacy. A two layer authentication using strong digital watermarking and cryptographic hashing is used to protect the integrity of shares. The proposed technique shows high resilience in stopping bit-flipping, cropping, and substitution attacks. Extensive experiments on multiple public face datasets show that the technique shows better FR accuracy, while eliminating share conspicuousness and guaranteeing integrity. The proposed framework sets a new standard for protecting facial data in such a way that privacy, security, and integrity are protected.

</details>


### [153] [Zero-shot 2D Grounding with Novel Affordance Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08929)
*Haomeng Zhang,Raymond A. Yeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出零样本2D可操作区域定位（NAT）的新任务及基准，并引入两个方法：训练免费的AffordAnything和可训练的AffordAnything+，后者在AGD20K-NAT基准上相比SOTA方法OOAL在IoU@0.4上提升12.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D可操作区域定位研究仅能识别训练中见过的可操作类型，缺乏对新颖可操作类型（novel affordance types）的泛化能力，而这对于真实世界应用至关重要。

Method: 提出NAT任务和基准；提出AffordAnything（基于分割线索的训练免费方法，利用可操作区域与物体子部分之间的强相关性）；进一步提出AffordAnything+（可训练变体，学习如何组合这些分割线索）。

Result: 在AGD20K-NAT基准上，AffordAnything+相比SOTA可操作区域定位方法OOAL在IoU@0.4上取得了12.3%的绝对提升。

Conclusion: 验证了利用分割线索实现零样本可操作区域泛化的有效性，AffordAnything系列方法能显著超越现有SOTA，为新颖可操作类型定位提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: 2D affordance grounding aims to locate the region of an object that a human can interact with. Existing research focuses on recognizing affordance types seen during training and does not study models' ability to generalize to novel affordances, which is crucial for real-world applications. We propose the task of zero-shot 2D grounding with novel affordance types (NAT) and introduce the NAT benchmarks. We then propose AffordAnything, a training-free method that leverages segmentation cues, motivated by the strong correlation between affordance regions and object subparts. To further improve performance, we develop AffordAnything+, a trainable variant that learns to combine these cues. On the proposed AGD20K-NAT benchmark, our best model AffordAnything+ achieves a substantial improvement of 12.3% (absolute) in IoU@0.4 over the SOTA affordance grounding method, OOAL.

</details>


### [154] [Can Webcam Gaze Constrain Mesa-Objectives in Driving Models? An Instrument Precision Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08947)
*Lennox Anderson,Ahmed Boutar,Jonah Mulcrone,Tal Erez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动驾驶中的危险检测系统可能产生"中间目标"（mesa objectives）——通过虚假相关性而非真正的危险识别来获得高训练性能。作者假设人类注视模式（通过WebGazer.js网络摄像头眼动追踪）可以作为特权信息来约束这一问题的形成。收集了138k帧级注视样本和388个行车记录仪片段进行实验，但所有实验均未发现注视信息带来的统计显著改进。几何分析表明根本原因在于WebGazer的定位误差（~130-257像素）超过了93%的检测危险目标尺寸（中位数36像素），在此仪器精度下无法实现物体级别的注视归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的危险检测系统可能发展出通过虚假相关性而非真正危险识别来获得高训练性能的中间目标，作者希望验证人类注视模式能否作为特权信息约束这种中间目标的形成，从而提升危险检测的真实泛化能力。

Method: 收集137,663帧级注视样本，与388个真实行车记录仪片段中的危险标注同步；设计两个标定协议（9点/45点击和11点/440点击）、两种模型架构（随机森林和因果Transformer）、每组实验5个随机种子并采用配对t检验；最后通过几何分析验证注视归因的物理可行性。

Result: 所有实验均未从注视信息中获得统计显著的改进（p = 0.919, 0.578, 0.667）。几何分析揭示根本原因：WebGazer的报错范围（~130-257像素）超过93%的检测危险目标尺寸（中位数36像素），在该仪器精度下物体级别的注视归因物理上不可行。

Conclusion: 实验证据表明，在WebGazer当前精度水平下，基于webcam的眼动追踪无法为自动驾驶危险检测提供有效的特权信息。研究结果为该方向的可行性提供了关键的负面证据，并指出要利用注视作为约束，需要更高精度的眼动追踪设备。

Abstract: Current hazard detection systems in autonomous driving may develop mesa objectives, learned internal goals that achieve high training performance through spurious correlations rather than genuine hazard recognition. We investigate whether human gaze patterns, captured via webcam-based eye tracking (WebGazer.js), can serve as privileged information to constrain mesa-objective formation. We collected 137,663 frame-level gaze samples synchronized with hazard annotations across 388 real dashcam clips, then test this hypothesis across two calibration protocols (9-point/45-click and 11-point/440-click), two model architectures (Random Forest and causal Transformer), and five random seeds per experiment with paired t-tests. No experiment yields a statistically significant improvement from gaze (p = 0.919, 0.578, and 0.667 respectively). A geometric analysis reveals the root cause: WebGazer's reported error (~130-257 px depending on configuration) exceeds 93% of detected hazard object sizes (median 36 px), rendering object-level gaze attribution physically impossible at this instrument precision.

</details>


### [155] [EndoMD-SLAM: Endoscopic Gaussian Splatting SLAM under Optical Degradation with Memory and Static-Transient Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08949)
*Nuo Chen,Kangqi Ni,Lulin Liu,Joga Ivatury,Ying Ding,Farshid Alambeigi,Tianlong Chen,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EndoMD-SLAM是一个面向内窥镜场景的密集3D重建框架，专门应对光学退化（如移动碎屑、水冲洗）导致的SLAM失败问题。通过记忆驱动的门控机制和自监督静态-瞬态分解，该框架在严重光学退化下可将绝对轨迹误差降低91%，渲染保真度提升9.9 dB PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯溅射SLAM系统依赖严格的多视图光度一致性，但临床内窥镜过程中存在间歇性光学退化（碎屑移动、水冲洗），导致系统错误地将摄像头附着的伪影融合进3D几何，造成跟踪漂移和不可逆的地图损坏。

Method: 1) 跟踪端：提出记忆驱动的门控机制，检测不可靠观测以暂停地图更新，并利用历史关键帧进行漂移感知的重定位。2) 建图端：采用自监督的静态-瞬态分解，将视觉污染物隔离到专用瞬态场中，防止伪影与持久解剖地图结构纠缠。3) 构建了面向退化的结肠镜视频基准用于系统评估。

Result: 在严重光学退化下，标准基线方法失败，而EndoMD-SLAM保持了几何完整性，绝对轨迹误差降低91%，渲染保真度提升9.9 dB PSNR。

Conclusion: EndoMD-SLAM通过专门的跟踪和建图机制有效解决了内窥镜场景中光学退化导致的多视图一致性失效问题，显著提升了退化条件下SLAM的稳定性和重建质量。

Abstract: Dense 3D reconstruction is critical for clinical endoscopic navigation and documentation. While Gaussian Splatting SLAM systems show promise in this domain, they fundamentally rely on strict multi-view photometric consistency. In routine procedures, this assumption is severely violated by intermittent optical degradations like moving debris and water flushing. Standard systems erroneously fuse these cameraattached artifacts into the persistent 3D geometry, causing severe tracking drift and irreversible map corruption. To address this limitation, we propose EndoMD-SLAM, a framework designed to maintain stability under optical degradation through specialized tracking and mapping mechanisms. On the tracking side, a memory-driven gating mechanism detects unreliable observations to suspend map updates and utilizes historical keyframes for drift-aware relocalization. On the mapping side, a self-supervised static-transient decomposition isolates visual contaminants into a dedicated transient field. This explicit separation prevents artifacts from structurally entangling with the persistent anatomical map. We curate a degradationfocused benchmark from colonoscopy videos to systematically evaluate these failure modes. Extensive experiments show that while standard baselines fail under severe optical degradation, EndoMD-SLAM preserves geometric integrity, reducing absolute trajectory error by 91% and improving rendering fidelity by 9.9 dB PSNR.

</details>


### [156] [Topology-Aware Global-Local Mamba Networks for Palm Vein Biometrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08951)
*Zhengxi Wu,Felix Marattukalam,Waleed H. Abdulla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个拓扑感知的全局-局部骨干网络用于掌静脉识别，结合多尺度局部特征、基于Sobel边缘先验的结构引导方向流和四方向状态空间扫描全局路径，在六个拓扑感知块中实现，通过分阶段门控融合整合三层表示。在HKPUNIR上达到99.13% top-1准确率和0.08% EER（7.2M参数），在VERA上达到92.42%准确率和0.61% EER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掌静脉识别属于细粒度生物识别任务，局部血管纹理和血管树全局布局都包含判别性信息，但公开数据集有限，需要在高参数效率下同时捕获局部纹理和全局结构信息的方法。

Method: 提出一个拓扑感知的全局-局部骨干网络，包含：(1) 多尺度局部特征提取；(2) 基于固定Sobel幅度边缘先验的结构引导方向流；(3) 四方向状态空间扫描的全局路径，这些组件集成在六个拓扑感知块(Topology-Aware Blocks)中；(4) 采用分阶段门控融合，按局部→结构→全局的顺序整合表示。

Result: 在HKPUNIR数据集上达到99.13% top-1准确率和0.08% EER，仅需7.2M参数；在VERA Palm Vein数据集上达到92.42%准确率和0.61% EER。在两个数据集上均以最小参数数实现了比其他方法（ResNet50, Vim-S, ViT-S, GLVM）更低的EER，但GLVM在top-1准确率上最强且在FLOPs上最便宜。

Conclusion: 提出的拓扑感知全局-局部骨干网络在参数效率方面具有优势，以最少的参数在两个掌静脉数据集上取得了最优的EER性能，证明了结合拓扑感知的多尺度局部特征、结构引导方向流和全局扫描路径能有效提升掌静脉识别的稳健性。

Abstract: Palm-vein recognition is a fine-grained biometric task in which both local vascular texture and the global layout of the vessel tree carry discriminative information, while public datasets remain limited. We propose a topology-aware global-local backbone that combines multi-scale local features, a structureguided directional stream built on a fixed Sobel-magnitude edge prior, and a four-direction state-space scan global pathway within six Topology-Aware Blocks. A staged gated fusion integrates local, structural, and global representations in that order. On HKPUNIR, our method achieves 99.13% top-1 accuracy and 0.08% EER with 7.2 M parameters; on VERA Palm Vein, it achieves 92.42% accuracy and 0.61% EER. Across both datasets it attains the lowest EER among ResNet50, Vim-S, ViT-S, and GLVM at the smallest parameter count, while GLVM remains the strongest in top-1 accuracy and the cheapest in FLOPs. Code is available upon request.

</details>


### [157] [Damage Classification for 3D Point Cloud Data via 3D Data Analysis and Vision Foundation Model-based 2D Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08955)
*Evan Perez,Kalelo Dukuray,Erika Ardiles-Cruz,Jie Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对比了两种点云数据损伤分类方法：基于3D PCD的损伤评估算法（3PDA，结合拓扑数据分析TDA与异常检测）和基于2D投影的损伤评估算法（2PDA，利用视觉基础模型VFM进行2D投影分类）。结果显示，3PDA精度更高但计算昂贵且仅适用于狭窄的物体几何类型；2PDA精度略低但计算效率提升一个数量级，且泛化能力更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D点云数据的细粒度损伤分类面临计算需求高和标注数据稀缺的双重挑战，需要探索兼顾精度与效率的可行方案。

Method: 提出两种方法：3PDA利用TDA对PointNet分割的3D点云组件进行紧致表示，结合异常检测算法量化结构退化；2PDA将3D点云投影到2D视图，利用大型视觉基础模型进行细粒度损伤检测。

Result: 3PDA在窄子集的物体几何上达到较高精度但计算成本高；2PDA精度略低但时间复杂度降低一个数量级，且能泛化到更广泛的物体类别。

Conclusion: 2D VFM管道是传统3PDA架构的高效轻量级替代方案，在细粒度损伤分类任务上表现良好，兼顾精度与资源效率。

Abstract: Fine-grained damage classification of 3D point cloud data (PCD) remains a persistent challenge, constrained by high computational demands and limited labeled data. This study examines two methods: 3D PCD-based damage assessment (3PDA) algorithm and 2D projection damage assessment (2PDA) In our 3PDA analysis algorithm, TDA is used to derive compact representations of 3D PCD segmented by pointNet, which are then integrated with anomaly detection algorithms to quantify structural degradation. We show that TDA effectively compresses geometric structure from VFM-segmented components into discriminative feature vectors and that anomaly detection models can reliably distinguish components with varying damage severity using only 3D PCD inputs. In the 2D projection analysis algorithm, we leverage large VFMs for granular damage detection by projecting 3D PCD into 2D views. These projections allow VFM based models to achieve competitive classification performance while requiring only a fraction of the computational cost associated with full 3D data processing. Our results demonstrate that 2D VFM pipelines in 2PDA can perform strongly on fine-grained damage classification tasks, highlighting their viability as lightweight, resource-efficient alternatives to traditional 3PDA architectures. Comparative evaluation shows that the 3PDA attains higher accuracy but only for a narrow subset of object geometries and at substantially higher computational cost due to its reliance on TDA and the scarcity of high-fidelity 3D datasets. In contrast, the 2PDA algorithm yields slightly lower accuracy but offers an order of magnitude reduction in time complexity and generalizes across a far broader range of object categories.

</details>


### [158] [RMR-Net: Degradation-Evidence-Guided Road-Image Restoration for Defect Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08957)
*Amir Ghorbani,Amirali K. Gostar,WeiQin Chuah,Vahid Ghorbani,Aidan Blair,Alireza Bab-Hadiashar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RMR-Net，一种紧凑的任务感知恢复前端网络，用于修复车载道路相机图像中的运动模糊、失焦、光照不良和噪声等退化问题，从而提升路面缺陷检测器的性能。在IVCNZ和PCM数据集上，RMR-Net在八个测试退化条件中取得七个最高mAP50，消融实验证明有界细节路径贡献最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载道路相机受运动模糊、离焦、光照差和噪声影响，会抹除薄裂缝和坑洞边界等路面缺陷检测器所需的关键细节，亟需一种有效的图像恢复前端来提升检测精度。

Method: RMR-Net是一个紧凑的任务感知恢复前端：从图像估计退化证据，可选地与现有退化上下文/参数融合，条件化轻量恢复模块，并通过有界残差路径返回高频路面细节。实验中使用保存的合成生成器参数作为条件信息，以干净训练的冻结YOLO11s检测器评估所有图像源。

Result: 在八个保留的退化条件下，RMR-Net在七个条件中取得最高mAP50，其中IVCNZ运动模糊为0.140-0.427，PCM离焦为0.060-0.233。消融实验显示有界细节路径是最大的局部贡献者，退化条件化和检测器感知稳定性项提供互补的引导。

Conclusion: RMR-Net证明紧凑的任务感知恢复前端能有效提升路面缺陷检测器在多种退化条件下的性能，其中高频细节恢复路径和退化条件化机制是关键设计要素。

Abstract: Vehicle-mounted road cameras are vulnerable to motion blur, defocus, poor illumination, and noise, which can erase thin cracks and pothole boundaries needed by road defect detectors. This paper presents RMR-Net, a compact task-aware restoration front end that estimates degradation evidence from the image, optionally fuses it with existing corruption context/parameters, conditions lightweight restoration blocks, and returns high-frequency pavement detail through a bounded residual path. The experimental scope is deliberately controlled: the conditioning information used on the Image and Vision Computing New Zealand (IVCNZ) pothole dataset and the Road Damage Dataset: Potholes, Cracks and Manholes (PCM) consists of saved synthetic-generator parameters, not measured vehicle telemetry. A clean-trained, frozen YOLO11s detector evaluates every image source. Across eight held-out degradation conditions, RMR-Net obtains the highest mAP50 in seven, including 0.140-0.427 for IVCNZ motion blur and 0.060-0.233 for PCM defocus. A compact ablation identifies the bounded detail path as the largest local contributor, while degradation conditioning and detector-aware stability terms provide complementary guidance.

</details>


### [159] [Fourier Self-Supervision for Fine-Grained Generalized Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08963)
*Sarah Rastegar,Mina Ghadimi Atigh,Pascal Mettes,Yuki M. Asano,Cees G. M. Snoek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于傅里叶变换的自监督方法（Fourier Self-Supervision），通过双频滤波策略（低通滤波提取抽象属性，高通滤波强调细节特征）来增强广义类别发现（GCD）中的细粒度判别能力，在多个细粒度数据集上超越现有最佳方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的广义类别发现方法通常基于自监督和对比学习，难以捕捉细粒度类别之间的微妙差异，往往依赖于表面视觉线索而忽略了人类分类时所依据的内在属性。

Method: 利用图像傅里叶变换，采用双频滤波策略：低通滤波器提取广泛抽象的高层类别属性，高通滤波器强调边缘和纹理等细粒度特征；每个滤波器作用于独立的潜在空间，其重叠表示共同构成更丰富完整的特征空间。

Result: 在多个细粒度数据集上的实验表明，该方法在未知类别数量的情况下仍然优于现有最先进方法，验证了其在广义类别发现中的有效性。

Conclusion: Fourier Self-Supervision通过双频特征提取有效增强了模型在细粒度类别发现中的判别能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery aims to recognize known categories while identifying novel ones within unlabeled data. Existing methods, typically based on self-supervision and contrastive learning, often struggle to capture fine-grained distinctions, relying on superficial visual cues rather than the intrinsic attributes humans use for categorization. We introduce Fourier Self-Supervision, that leverages the Fourier transform of images to enhance the discrimination of subtle differences and support the discovery of new categories. Our method employs a dual frequency filtering strategy: a low-pass filter first extracts broad, abstract attributes that capture high-level category information, while a high-pass filter emphasizes fine details such as edges and textures that are essential for fine-grained recognition. Each operates on a dedicated latent space, and their overlapping representations together yield a richer, more complete feature space. This dual-frequency approach not only refines feature extraction to identify novel categories, but also strengthens the model's discriminative power in fine-grained category discovery. Experiments on multiple fine-grained datasets show that incorporating Fourier Self-Supervision outperforms state-of-the-art methods, even when the number of classes is unknown, demonstrating its effectiveness for Generalized Category Discovery. Our code is available at: https://github.com/SarahRastegar/FourEx.

</details>


### [160] [Detecting Clear Contact Lenses for Iris Recognition: A Two-Stage Mask-Guided Attention Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08977)
*Parisa Farmanifard,Arun Ross*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了透明隐形眼镜对虹膜识别的影响与检测问题，提出两阶段检测框架（Stage 1 检测图案镜片，Stage 2 使用带 Mask-Guided Spatial Attention 的 ConvNeXt-Base 区分透明镜片与无镜片），并在检测到透明镜片时通过 z-score 校准方法调整 VeriEye 匹配分数，在四个数据集上实现了 90.0%-98.8% 的检测准确率，并将 EER 降低了 4.1%-28.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明隐形眼镜广泛使用，但由于其透明无纹理特征，在虹膜识别领域鲜受关注，且常被假设为对识别无影响。作者旨在检验这一假设，并解决透明镜片难以检测的问题，从而改善虹膜识别性能。

Method: 首先使用商业 VeriEye 匹配器在四个基准数据集上评估透明镜片对虹膜识别的影响；然后提出两阶段检测框架：Stage 1 使用现有 PAD 模型识别图案镜片，Stage 2 使用配备 Mask-Guided Spatial Attention (MGSA) 的 ConvNeXt-Base 模型区分透明镜片与无镜片，MGSA 融合 Hough 衍生的解剖 ROI 掩码、学习空间注意力和 Squeeze-and-Excitation 通道重校准；最后引入 z-score 校准方法调整检测到透明镜片时的匹配分数。

Result: 透明镜片会轻微降低真实匹配分数并增加验证错误；完整的两阶段检测框架在四个数据集上达到 90.0%-98.8% 的准确率；z-score 校准方法将 EER 降低了 4.1%-28.3%。

Conclusion: 透明隐形眼镜确实会对虹膜识别产生负面影响，通过可靠的透明镜片检测和分数校准可以有效提升虹膜验证性能，填补了该领域对透明镜片关注不足的空白。

Abstract: This work focuses on the impact and detection of clear contact lenses in the context of iris recognition. While the detection of cosmetic or patterned contact lenses has been extensively studied under the presentation attack detection (PAD) paradigm, clear prescription contact lenses, that are typically transparent, have received comparatively less attention despite their widespread use. Unlike patterned lenses, clear lenses introduce no salient texture artifact, making them difficult to detect and are often assumed to have no impact on iris recognition. We first examine this assumption using the commercial VeriEye matcher on four benchmark datasets and show that clear lenses marginally degrade genuine match scores and increase verification error. We then propose a two-stage contact-lens detection framework. Stage~1 uses an existing PAD model to identify patterned lenses, while Stage~2 focuses on the more challenging clear-lens versus no-lens distinction using a ConvNeXt-Base model equipped with Mask-Guided Spatial Attention (MGSA). The proposed MGSA module incorporates a Hough-derived anatomical ROI mask together with learned spatial attention and Squeeze-and-Excitation channel recalibration, allowing the network to focus on subtle limbal cues associated with clear lens wear. Across four datasets, the full pipeline consisting of both patterned and clear contact lens detection achieves between 90.0\%--98.8\% accuracy. Finally, we introduce a z-score calibration method that adjusts VeriEye match scores when a clear lens is detected in the input images. This calibration reduces EER by 4.1\%--28.3\% across datasets, demonstrating that reliable clear contact lens detection can directly improve iris verification performance.

</details>


### [161] [DeepFreqMark: End-To-End Learnable Frequency-Domain Watermarking with Spherical Attack Simulation for Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08999)
*Chen-Hsiu Huang,Mario Köppen,Ja-Ling Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeepFreqMark是一个端到端可学习的频域水印框架，通过神经网络编码器和解码器替代传统手工几何图案水印，并引入基于Slerp的攻击模拟来规避DDIM反转的高计算开销，在真实攻击下实现更低的误码率并支持256比特消息容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的频域水印方法依赖手工设计的几何图案嵌入到初始潜在噪声中，存在容量有限和图案设计僵化的问题，难以应对AI生成图像带来的版权侵权和虚假信息风险。

Method: 提出端到端可学习的频域水印框架DeepFreqMark，使用神经消息编码器和解码器替代手工图案工程；为规避训练中DDIM反转的计算瓶颈，引入基于Slerp的攻击模拟方法，该方案直接在噪声潜在空间操作并严格保持高斯方差。

Result: 大量实验表明DeepFreqMark在真实世界攻击下相比基线方法实现了显著更低的比特错误率（BER），并可扩展至256比特消息容量。

Conclusion: DeepFreqMark通过端到端学习和高效的攻击模拟策略，在频域水印的容量、鲁棒性和实用性方面取得了显著提升，为LDM生成图像的版权保护和溯源提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: The proliferation of AI-generated images produced by Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) has raised critical concerns regarding copyright infringement and misinformation. Although existing frequency-domain watermarking methods embed handcrafted geometric patterns into the initial latent noise prior to generation, they suffer from limited capacity and rigid pattern designs. We propose DeepFreqMark, an end-to-end learnable frequency-domain watermarking framework that replaces manual pattern engineering with a neural message encoder and decoder. To circumvent the computational bottleneck caused by Denoising Diffusion Implicit Model (DDIM) inversion during training, we introduce a Spherical Linear Interpolation (Slerp)-based attack simulation. This approach operates directly on the noise latent while strictly preserving the Gaussian variance. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DeepFreqMark achieves significantly lower Bit Error Rates (BER) than baseline methods under real-world attacks and scales to 256 bits message capacity. Our source code is available at https://github.com/chenhsiu48/DeepFreqMark.

</details>


### [162] [SignLlama: Enhancing Gloss-free Sign Language Translation by Prioritizing Visual Features for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09006)
*Shiwei Gan,Xiao Liu,Yafeng Yin,Zhiwei Jiang,Bowen Guo,Lie Xie,Sanglu Lu,Hongkai Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SignLlama，一种无需额外标注或外部数据集即可将大语言模型（LLM）适配到无词汇表手语翻译（GFSLT）任务的方法，通过过滤伪词汇CTC预训练和视觉优先蒸馏训练策略解决视觉-文本特征分布差异及模型过度依赖文本输入的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在GFSLT任务中面临两个关键挑战：(1) 视觉特征与文本特征之间存在固有的分布差异，使LLM难以理解视觉输入；(2) 现有方法在自回归框架中拼接视觉和文本特征，导致模型过度关注文本输入而忽视视觉线索，因为LLM主要在文本数据上预训练。

Method: 提出两种方法：(1) Filtered Pseudo-Gloss CTC Pretraining——利用从文本序列生成的过滤伪词汇序列来监督视觉骨干网络的训练；(2) Visual-Prioritized Distillation——定义一条仅依赖视觉输入的视觉-only预测路径（文本输入被掩码），并将标准视觉-文本预测路径的输出蒸馏到该路径中，鼓励模型优先关注视觉特征。

Result: 在多个GFSLT数据集上取得了具有竞争力的性能，且无需使用任何额外模态或外部手语数据集进行预训练。综合实验和定性分析验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SignLlama通过缓解视觉-文本特征分布差距和纠正模型对文本输入的过度依赖，成功实现了LLM在无词汇表手语翻译任务中的有效适应，证明了所提方法的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success across a wide range of tasks. However, fine-tuning LLMs for Gloss-Free Sign Language Translation (GFSLT) remains a challenge. In this paper, we investigate how to effectively adapt LLMs to the GFSLT task. We show that there are two key issues that need to be solved: (1) the inherent distributional gap between visual feature inputs and text feature inputs makes it difficult for LLMs to interpret visual inputs; and (2) existing approaches typically concatenate visual and textual features in an autoregressive framework, which leads to the model overemphasizing textual inputs and deprioritizing visual cues, as LLMs are pretrained predominantly on text-centric data. To address the first challenge, we propose a simple yet effective method named Filtered Pseudo-Gloss CTC Pretraining, which leverages filtered pseudo-gloss sequences generated from text sequences to supervise the training of the visual backbone. To tackle the second issue, we introduce a Visual-Prioritized Distillation training strategy. Specifically, we define a visual-only prediction path in which text inputs are masked, and the model is required to generate the target sequence relying solely on visual inputs. To guide this path, the outputs from the standard visual-textual prediction are then distilled into the visual-only prediction path, encouraging the model to prioritize visual features. Comprehensive experiments and qualitative analyses demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed model. The proposed SignLlama achieves very competitive performance on multiple datasets for GFSLT tasks, without using any extra modalities or external sign language datasets for pretraining.

</details>


### [163] [GeoAI-based post-segmentation quality validation of building footprints via spatial feature engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09048)
*Shah Imran Ahsan Chowdhury,Kazi Jihadur Rashid,Rajsree Das Tuli,Rahul Saha,Bulbul Ahammad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向深度学习建筑足迹提取的多域GeoAI质量控制框架，利用机器学习分类器自动检测和纠正如矢量拓扑不一致等错误，在孟加拉国五个无人机航测站点上验证，最终将数据库错误率从27.32%降至4.62%，纯度提升至95.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习从高分辨率影像中提取建筑足迹时常常产生拓扑不一致的矢量结果，无法直接用于GIS数据库，需要一种自动化的质量控制与错误检测机制来净化矢量足迹数据库。

Method: 使用U-Net（ResNet-34）和SAM-LoRA（ViT-B）生成候选足迹，经矢量化、几何正则化和空间互斥约束消除重复。利用24个预测变量（几何、空间上下文、光谱和纹理特征），训练机器学习分类器（如决策树），在开发分区训练并在空间独立的测试集上验证。

Result: 决策树分类器在未见测试站点上达到95.31%的准确率、91.06%的F1分数和0.880的MCC。框架识别出87.34%的错误足迹，保留98.31%的正确结构，将残余错误率从27.32%降至4.62%，数据库纯度提升至95.38%，相对误差降低83.09%。

Conclusion: 分割后的对象级机器学习为生产级GIS工作流提供了一种高度可迁移、稳健的自动化质量保障机制，有效提升建筑足迹数据库的可用性和准确性。

Abstract: Deep learning-based building footprint extraction from high-resolution imagery often produces topologically inconsistent vectors unfit for direct GIS database ingestion. To address this, we present a multidomain GeoAI quality control framework that automates error detection to systematically purify vector footprint databases. Candidate footprints were generated across five UAV survey sites in Bangladesh using U-Net (ResNet-34) and SAM-LoRA (ViT-B). The extracted raster masks were vectorized, geometrically regularized, and consolidated under a spatial-exclusivity constraint to eliminate duplicate representations. We used twenty-four predictors capturing geometric, spatial-contextual, and raster-derived spectral and texture properties. Machine Learning (ML) classifiers were trained on a development partition (Sites B-D) and rigorously validated on a spatially independent test set (Site E) excluded from hyperparameter tuning and class balancing. The experimental results demonstrate that geometric and spatial-contextual predictors using Decision Tree (DT) provide the most effective discriminatory evidence for identifying object-level boundary deformations. DT achieved an accuracy of 95.31%, an F1-score of 91.06%, and a Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) of 0.880 on the unseen testing site. At the database level, this framework successfully identified 87.34% of erroneous footprints while maintaining 98.31% of acceptable structures, reducing the residual error proportion from 27.32% to 4.62% and improving final database purity to 95.38%. This translates into a relative error reduction of 83.09%. The findings indicate that post-segmentation object-level ML provides a highly transferable, robust mechanism for automated quality assurance in production-ready geographic information system (GIS) workflows.

</details>


### [164] [Triple Expert Learning from Noisy Labels for Semi-Supervised Vision Foundation Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09052)
*Xuanyu Liu,Zheng Fang,Hongyang He,Yundi Hong,Daizong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TriNoL是一种用于半监督视觉基础模型适配的三专家学习框架，将无标签样本按置信度分为三组，分别路由到三个专门的LoRA专家（高置信度正样本专家、中等置信度对齐专家、低置信度负样本专家），以提升对伪标签噪声的鲁棒性，同时保持低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型（VFM）的半监督适配通常冻结骨干网络并更新LoRA等轻量模块，但伪标签的可靠性参差不齐，单一LoRA适配器不得不在同一个低秩空间中吸收可靠、模糊和含噪声的梯度，导致VFM适配对伪标签噪声敏感。

Method: TriNoL将无标签样本划分到三个置信度区域，分别分配给三个LoRA专家：Positive Expert处理高置信度伪标签，Alignment Expert处理中等置信度模糊样本，Negative Expert处理低置信度含噪样本。VFM骨干保持冻结，仅更新LoRA专家和分类头。

Result: 通过将不同伪标签可靠性区域分离到专门的适配路径中，TriNoL提高了对噪声监督的鲁棒性，同时保持较低的训练成本。

Conclusion: TriNoL通过三专家分离机制有效缓解了半监督VFM适配中的伪标签噪声问题，在不增加显著训练开销的前提下提升了适配鲁棒性。

Abstract: Semi-supervised adaptation of vision foundation models (VFMs) commonly freezes the pretrained backbone and updates lightweight modules such as LoRA. However, pseudo-labels have mixed reliability, and a single LoRA adapter must absorb reliable, ambiguous, and noisy gradients in the same low-rank space. This can make VFM adaptation sensitive to pseudo-label noise. We propose \textbf{TriNoL}, a \textbf{Tri}ple-expert learning framework from \textbf{No}isy \textbf{L}abels for semi-supervised VFM adaptation. TriNoL routes unlabeled samples into three confidence regions and assigns them to three LoRA experts: a Positive Expert for high-confidence pseudo-labels, an Alignment Expert for medium-confidence ambiguous samples, and a Negative Expert for low-confidence noisy samples. The VFM backbone remains frozen, and only the LoRA experts and classifier head are updated. By separating different pseudo-label reliability regions into specialized adaptation paths, TriNoL improves robustness to noisy supervision while keeping the training cost low.

</details>


### [165] [Model the Edit, Not the Image: Visual Autoregressive Editing from a Source-Centric Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09057)
*Hongyi Fang,Chuwen Xie,Benjia Zhou,Yu-Xuan Qiu,Chenggong Hu,Zhibin Wang,Chao Chen,Jianbin Qin,Rui Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EditMod，一种基于源码中心视角的视觉自回归模型（VAR）文本引导图像编辑方法，通过比较源和目标条件下的预测差异作为编辑方向，对源token进行残差更新，在保持高保真度的同时实现快速端到端编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练免费的VAR编辑方法采用生成中心的范式，将编辑视为目标条件下的再生成，未充分利用VAR的多尺度源表示，且可能引入额外计算或用户干预。作者希望通过源中心的视角更高效地利用VAR的结构化源信息。

Method: EditMod在共享的自回归上下文下比较源条件和目标条件下的预测，将它们之间的差异视为尺度级的编辑方向，并以此作为残差更新应用到选定尺度的源token上，从而实现对源图像的条件诱导修改。

Result: EditMod在源图像保真度和文本对齐方面均达到了领先水平，且在单个A100 GPU上仅需1.57秒即可完成1K图像的端到端编辑，无需每张图像的预处理步骤。

Conclusion: 源码中心视角比生成中心范式更适合VAR图像编辑任务，EditMod展示了在该框架下兼顾编辑质量与效率的可行性。

Abstract: Next-scale visual autoregressive models (VARs) have emerged as a powerful generative paradigm, producing high-quality images through efficient coarse-to-fine prediction. However, their potential for text-guided image editing remains largely underexplored. Existing training-free VAR editing approaches often formulate editing as target-conditioned regeneration guided or constrained by the source image, and may rely on inversion, test-time optimization, attention control, or user-provided masks. This generation-centric formulation does not fully exploit the multiscale source representations provided by VARs and may introduce additional computation or intervention. We instead take a source-centric perspective on VAR editing, in which the encoded source image tokens serve as the primary visual state and the editing process focuses on condition-induced changes. Based on this perspective, we propose \textbf{EditMod}, which compares source- and target-conditioned predictions under a shared autoregressive context, treats their difference as a scale-wise editing direction, and applies it as a residual update to source tokens at selected scales. Experiments show that EditMod achieves leading source-image fidelity while maintaining strong text alignment, and completes end-to-end editing of a 1K image in only 1.57 seconds on a single A100 GPU without per-image preparation.

</details>


### [166] [Learning human joint torques from pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09083)
*Chen Chen,Rui Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了VID，一个基于视觉的逆动力学数据集和基准，用于直接从真实单目图像预测人体关节力矩。VID包含63,369帧同步数据，并提出VID-Network模型，在整体关节力矩估计上取得mPJE为1.7612 N·m/kg，比最优基线提升39.81%，是首个面向视觉驱动人体逆动力学的实用基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人体关节力矩估计方法依赖于表面肌电、动捕标记、力板或仿真模仿数据，难以应用于普通RGB图像。作者希望将生物力学分析从受控实验室环境推广到真实世界场景，因此需要建立直接基于真实单目图像进行逆动力学推断的方法。

Method: 作者提出了VID数据集和VID-Network模型。VID包含63,369帧同步的真实人体图像、运动学标注、人体测量属性和OpenSim导出的动力学标签。VID-Network结合了姿态预训练的空间概率特征、标记点回归和时序力矩推断，从图像序列中恢复关节力矩。

Result: 在VID基准上，VID-Network实现了整体mPJE为1.7612 N·m/kg，较最佳对比基线提升了39.81%，在所有评估的关节类型和大部分动作类别中均获得最低误差。

Conclusion: VID建立了首个面向视觉驱动人体逆动力学的实用基准，为在较少受约束环境中研究生物力学推断提供了基础，推动了生物力学分析从实验室走向真实世界场景。

Abstract: Estimating human joint torques from visual observations is a key step toward bringing biomechanical analysis from controlled laboratories to real-world movement scenarios. Existing torque estimation methods typically depend on surface electromyography, motion-capture markers, force plates, or simulated imitation data, which limits their applicability to ordinary RGB images. In this work, we introduce VID, a vision-based inverse dynamics dataset and benchmark for predicting human joint torques directly from real monocular images. VID contains 63,369 synchronized frames with real human images, kinematic annotations, anthropometric attributes, and OpenSim-derived dynamic labels, providing paired visual and biomechanical supervision for real-image inverse dynamics. We further define a standardized evaluation protocol covering overall torque estimation, joint-specific analysis, and action-specific prediction. To establish a strong reference model, we propose VID-Network, which combines pose-pretrained spatial probabilistic features, marker regression, and temporal torque inference to recover joint torques from image sequences. Experiments on VID show that VID-Network achieves an overall mPJE of 1.7612 N$\cdot$m/kg, improving over the best compared baseline by 39.81\%, and obtains the lowest error across all evaluated joint types and most action categories. VID establishes a first practical benchmark for vision-driven human inverse dynamics and provides a foundation for studying biomechanical inference in less constrained environments.

</details>


### [167] [TLDChoiceNet: Quantitatively Choosing a Transfer Learning Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09091)
*Jing Ning,James D. Braza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TLDChoiceNet模型，用于从多个候选数据集中选择最佳的迁移学习预训练数据集。通过预测微调后的测试集准确率，模型能以0.031的MSE精准选择最优数据集，并设计了两个无监督指标（DD和ACC）来解释和预测迁移学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏一种定量方法来选择最佳的迁移学习数据集。虽然直觉上预训练数据集应比微调数据集拥有更多类别和每类样本，但没有量化标准来指导选择。

Method: 设计了TLDChoiceNet模型，包含两个版本：简单版本（v1）和利用ImageNet预训练ResNet50 v2嵌入并融合每类信息的版本（v2）。此外提出了两个无监督指标：分布距离（DD）和平均类相关性（ACC），用于选择最优迁移学习数据集。

Result: 简单版本TLDChoiceNet在测试集上达到0.154的MSE；版本2利用ImageNet预训练嵌入后，MSE降低5倍至0.031。DD指标对微调准确率线性回归的R2为0.89，ACC指标将R2提升至0.97。

Conclusion: 数据集的低层统计特征可以解释迁移学习效果，使用ImageNet预训练可以将不同类别在潜在特征空间中嵌入得更远，从而提升迁移学习数据集的选择准确性。

Abstract: Transfer learning is particularly useful in settings with limited training data, and within image classification it is common to transfer learn upon massive datasets like ImageNet , CIFAR-100, or COCO . Qualitatively, it seems a transfer learning dataset should have both more classes and more examples per class than the fine tuning dataset; however, a quantitative method to choose the best transfer learning dataset does not currently exist. In this paper, we design TLDChoiceNet, a model to choose the best transfer learning dataset given a fine tuning dataset by predicting the test-set accuracy after fine-tuning. A simple version 1 achieves 0.154 MSE on the test dataset, while a version 2 leveraging an ImageNet pre-trained ResNet50 v2 embedding with per-class information attains a 5X lower MSE of 0.031. We further design two metrics that enable an unsupervised method of choosing an optimal transfer learning dataset: distribution distance (DD), which linearly regresses against fine-tune accuracy with an R2 of 0.89, and average class correlation (ACC), which improves the R2 to 0.97. Our results underscore that a dataset's low-level statistics can explain the transfer learning effect, and that using a pre-trained ImageNet can embed different classes further apart in latent feature space.

</details>


### [168] [SI-Edit: Toward Sketch-Instruction Guided Local Image Editing with Pixel-Level Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09097)
*Weixin Ye,Wei Wang,Hongguang Zhu,Xuecheng Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SI-Data数据集和SI-Edit框架，解决基于草图图像编辑中像素级精度不足的问题。SI-Data通过MLLM自动化管线生成包含原始图像、局部几何草图、语义指令和编辑后图像的四元组数据；SI-Edit框架将语义指令与几何约束协同整合，并建立了结构保真度和语义遵循度的评估指标体系。实验表明SI-Edit在结构控制和像素级局部编辑方面优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于草图的图像编辑在精细局部变形方面缺乏像素级精度，其根本原因是缺少同时提供几何约束和语义指令的高质量公开基准数据集，阻碍了该领域的发展。

Method: 1）提出SI-Data：利用MLLM自动化管线合成包含原始图像、局部几何草图、语义指令和编辑图像的四元组数据；2）提出SI-Edit：协同整合语义指令与精确几何约束的编辑框架；3）建立涵盖结构保真度（如草图到边缘对齐）和语义遵循度的综合评价指标。

Result: 实验结果显示，SI-Edit相比基线方法在基于草图的图像编辑中提供了更可靠的结构控制，实现了符合用户意图的精确、像素级局部细化。

Conclusion: SI-Data和SI-Edit有效解决了草图编辑中几何与语义协同的关键问题，填补了高质量基准数据的空白，数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: Despite rapid advances in generative models, achieving pixel-level precision in sketch-based image editing remains a persistent challenge, particularly for fine-grained local deformations. This gap stems primarily from the critical shortage of high-quality, publicly available benchmark datasets that jointly provide geometric constraints and semantic instructions. To address this issue, we first introduce **SI-Data**, a high-quality dataset specifically designed for instruction-guided local sketch editing. We develop an automated pipeline leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to synthesize comprehensive quadruplets comprising original images, local geometric sketches, semantic instructions, and corresponding edited images. By providing both reliable spatial anchors and explicit semantic intent, SI-Data uniquely enables collaborative spatial-semantic learning. Building upon this, we propose a collaborative framework called **SI-Edit** that integrates semantic instructions with precise geometric constraints. Furthermore, to address the lack of standardized evaluation, we establish a comprehensive set of metrics designed to measure both structural fidelity (e.g., sketch-to-edge alignment) and semantic adherence. Experimental results demonstrate that SI-Edit provides more reliable structural control than baselines for sketch-based image editing, and achieves precise, pixel-level local refinements aligned with user intent. The data and code are released on the [project page](https://github.com/ywxsuperstar/SIEdit).

</details>


### [169] [Contrastive Mask Fidelity: Reference-Free Auditing of Ground-Truth Masks in Remote Sensing Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09101)
*Shuaishuai Cao,Shuwei Peng,Meng Tang,Min Huang,Youjin Wang,Jie Chen,Jing Ouyang,Zhiwei Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出对比掩码保真度（CMF），一种免训练、免参考的图像语义分割掩码质量评估指标，利用冻结的视觉-语言模型对候选掩码进行"保留/擦除"反事实对比验证，并在10371个图像-类别对上审计了十个遥感数据集的标注质量，揭示出类别相关的系统性标注偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感图像的语义分割标注往往粗糙、不完整或错位，现有高重叠分数可能只是反映了与不完美标注的一致性而非与真实图像的忠实度，构成"评估悖论"。需要一种直接以图像证据为参照的质量评估方法。

Method: CMF通过构造每个掩码的保留与擦除两种反事实视图，交由冻结的视觉-语言评判模型判断类别证据是否集中在掩码内部且外部缺失。文中用Seg-Probe（基于SegEarth-OV3的免训练开放词汇探针）生成候选掩码，在十个遥感基准数据集上对10,731个图像-类别对进行审计，并通过盲法三位标注者共识验证CMF判断。

Result: 在受控掩码损坏实验中验证有效；审计发现人工建造类（建筑、道路、汽车）在62-85%的配对中更倾向于候选掩码，而模糊土地覆盖类型更常偏向人类标注。在盲法三位标注者共识上，CMF在81%的配对中与专家判断一致，优于仅保留评分、模型置信度和训练后的标签质量基线。基于保守类别仲裁的监督信号改善了跨域迁移性能。

Conclusion: CMF可作为可扩展工具用于审计ground truth而非预设其完美，揭示不同类别存在系统性标注偏差，并通过保守仲裁改善下游迁移性能，为遥感分割标注质量评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation models are trained and evaluated against human-drawn masks, yet remote-sensing annotations are often coarse, incomplete, or misaligned; high overlap scores may then reflect agreement with imperfect labels rather than faithfulness to the image, creating an evaluation paradox. We introduce Contrastive Mask Fidelity (CMF), a training-free, reference-free metric that scores competing class masks directly against image evidence. CMF composites keep and erase counterfactual views of each mask and asks a frozen vision-language judge whether class evidence is concentrated inside the mask and absent outside. We validate CMF on controlled mask corruptions, then audit 10,731 image-class pairs across ten remote-sensing benchmarks using candidate masks from Seg-Probe, a training-free open-vocabulary probe built on SegEarth-OV3 that outperforms prior baselines on nine of ten datasets. The audit reveals systematic, class-dependent annotation distortion: man-made classes such as buildings, roads, and cars favor the candidate mask on 62-85% of pairs, whereas ambiguous land cover more often favors human annotations. On a blinded three-annotator consensus, CMF matches expert judgment on 81% of pairs, exceeding keep-only scoring, model confidence, and a trained label-quality baseline. Finally, conservative class-wise arbitration yields supervision that improves cross-domain transfer over raw annotations and matched replacement controls, positioning CMF as a scalable tool for auditing ground truth rather than presuming it infallible.

</details>


### [170] [View-Adaptive Renderer for View-Consistent 2D-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09110)
*U-Chae Jun,Jaeeun Ko,Jiwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种视图自适应的神经渲染框架，能够从部分不一致的多视图输入中稳健地重建3D形状，通过视图自适应神经渲染器和自注意力融合模块在保证准确性的同时降低计算负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单目3D生成管线通常先合成多视图再基于NeRF重建，但投影歧义会导致生成视点间出现视觉不连续，进而影响重建精度。现有解决方案要么计算开销大，要么无法充分解决视图间的不一致性问题。

Method: 提出视图自适应神经渲染框架：1) 视图自适应神经渲染器独立纠正视点相关误差，同时共享全局特征主干以保持结构一致性；2) 自注意力融合模块自适应整合多视图信息，确保几何一致性；3) 主要依赖光度渲染损失和轻量注意力正则化器，无需扩散模型的SDS监督。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法持续提升了3D重建保真度，在不使用基于扩散的SDS监督的情况下达到接近最先进的性能，同时保持较高的计算效率。

Conclusion: 该方法在准确性和效率之间取得了良好平衡，使其非常适合实际应用中的3D重建任务。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D shapes from a single image remains a fundamental yet challenging problem in computer vision. Traditional monocular 3D generation pipelines typically synthesize multiple views from a single input image before applying Neural Radiance Field (NeRF)-based reconstruction. However, inherent projective ambiguities often produce visual discontinuities across generated viewpoints, leading to inaccuracies in reconstructed 3D models. Current solutions either incur significant additional computational burdens or fail to adequately resolve practical inconsistencies between synthesized views. To address these limitations, we propose a novel viewpoint-adaptive neural rendering framework that enables robust 3D reconstruction even when given partially inconsistent multi-view inputs. Our approach introduces view-adaptive neural renderers that independently correct viewpoint-dependent errors while simultaneously sharing a global feature backbone to preserve structural coherence. Furthermore, we propose a self-attention fusion module that adaptively integrates multi-view information, ensuring geometric consistency without relying heavily on indirect regularizations or computationally intensive methods. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that our method consistently improves 3D reconstruction fidelity. Importantly, our approach achieves near state-of-the-art performance without diffusion-based SDS supervision, relying primarily on photometric rendering loss with lightweight attention regularizers. This balance between accuracy and efficiency makes the proposed framework highly practical for real-world applications.

</details>


### [171] [Visual Distortion Detection in UGC Images Using Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09122)
*Ziheng Jia,Yingji Liang,Jiaying Qian,Xiongkuo Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VIGIL，一种基于大语言模型（LMM）架构的视觉失真检测方法。通过构建包含140K失真图像的VIGIL-140K训练集，利用LLM解码器多层特征作为多检测器进行同步检测，并保留非失真类的预测线索来缓解合成到真实（S2A）场景中的前景-背景分离问题，在合成失真检测和S2A任务上均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大型多模态模型的图像质量评估方法主要依赖文本驱动的监督微调，存在检测精度不足的问题；同时，合成失真图像在真实场景中泛化能力差，面临合成到真实（S2A）的关键挑战。

Method: 1) 从超过1000K候选样本中构建VIGIL-140K训练集，包含140K+失真图像，经过严格质量过滤和精心设计的失真注入，覆盖8种主要合成失真类别。2) 利用LLM解码器不同层作为多个检测器，使用多级特征进行同步失真检测。3) 保留非失真类别的预测线索，缓解S2A问题中的前景-背景模糊分离。

Result: 经过后处理后，VIGIL在域内合成失真检测和S2A任务上均一致优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: VIGIL通过多检测器架构和利用非失真类线索的策略，有效解决了LMM在IQA中的检测精度不足和S2A泛化问题，为基于LMM的图像质量评估提供了新的训练范式和思路。

Abstract: The localized depiction of perceptual quality has long been a crucial, yet underexplored, challenge in image quality assessment (IQA). Existing approaches based on large multimodal models (LMMs) predominantly rely on text-driven supervised fine-tuning (SFT).
  However, this training paradigm exhibits notable limitations in detection accuracy. Moreover, synthetically distorted images, which are often used as the primary training data source,
  show a significant generalization gap when deployed in real-world scenarios; thus, the \textbf{synthetic-to-authentic (\textit{S2A})} problem represents a critical challenge. Motivated by these issues, we propose \textbf{\textit{VIGIL}}, which leverages the LMM architecture for precise visual distortion detection. From a candidate pool of over 1000K samples, we construct the \textbf{\textit{VIGIL-140K}} training set, which consists of over 140K distorted images. These images are obtained through rigorous quality filtering and carefully crafted distortion injection, covering 8 major synthetic distortion categories.
  Our model leverages different layers of the large language model (LLM) decoder, treating them as \textit{multiple detectors} that perform synchronous distortion detection using multi-level features. Additionally, we retain distortion cues from predictions assigned to the non-distortion class, which helps mitigate the ambiguous foreground-background (\textit{FG-BG}) separation commonly encountered in the \textit{S2A} problem.
  After post-processing, our model consistently outperforms strong baselines on both in-domain synthetic distortion detection and \textit{S2A} tasks.

</details>


### [172] [When Latents Forget Pixels: Restoring Fidelity in Diffusion Transformer Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09133)
*Yu Shi,Yuyao Zhang,Yu-wing Tai*

Main category: cs.CV
