TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了32种基础模型（FMs）在人脸呈现攻击检测（PAD）中的表现，发现零样本提示方法接近随机水平，而低秩适配（LoRA）在数据集内表现优异（ACER低于2%），但跨数据集泛化能力仍然有限，表明预训练表征和适配数据比轻量适配策略对跨数据集泛化影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PAD方法在跨数据集评估下性能严重退化（从近乎完美降至近乎随机），而当前PAD系统主要聚焦于CLIP基础模型，忽略了具有不同架构和训练过程的其他基础模型的潜力。

Method: 系统评估32种不同架构和训练方式的基础模型，使用零样本提示和低秩适配（LoRA，少于1%可训练权重）两种策略，在MCIO基准（MSU-MFSD、CASIA-FASD、Replay-Attack、OULU-NPU）上进行数据集内和跨数据集评估。

Result: 零样本提示在各类模型和规模下性能接近随机水平；LoRA适配的视觉编码器在大多数数据集内ACER低于2%，但跨数据集ACER显著更高。LoRA主要优化的是数据集内的决策边界，而非提升跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 预训练表征和适配数据集的选择对跨数据集PAD泛化起主导作用，轻量适配策略（如LoRA）对泛化的贡献有限，未来研究应更多关注如何提升基础模型表征的跨域鲁棒性和适配数据的多样性。

Abstract: Face presentation attack detection (PAD) aims to reliably detect a wide range of presentation attacks. While PAD methods achieve strong performance within individual datasets, their performance degrades under cross-dataset evaluation. Variations in sensors or lighting conditions can reduce the effectiveness of detectors from near-perfect to nearly random. Foundation models (FMs) have emerged as a promising alternative because typical PAD datasets, such as the MCIO benchmarks (MSU-MFSD, CASIA-FASD, Replay-Attack, and OULU-NPU), are small relative to the scale used for web-based pretraining. However, existing PAD systems primarily focus on CLIP-based foundation models, while overlooking other FMs with different architectures and training procedures. This study addresses this question by systematically evaluating 32 FMs. Zero-shot prompting achieves performance near chance across model families and scales. The vision encoders, when low-rankadapted (LoRA) with fewer than 1% trainable weights, achieve below 2% intra-dataset ACER in most cases, while cross-dataset ACER is substantially higher. LoRA primarily refines the decision boundary within a dataset, suggesting that pretrained representations and the adaptation dataset play a larger role in cross-dataset generalization than the evaluated lightweight adaptation strategy.

</details>


### [241] [NeuroRefiner: Morphology-Aware Multi-Agent Refinement for 3D Fluorescence Microscopy Neuron Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09636)
*Haiyang Yan,Jinyue Guo,Yanchao Zhang,Bingqing Wang,Zhenchen Li,Jing Liu,Jiazheng Liu,Linlin Li,Hua Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuroRefiner，一个用于荧光显微镜3D神经元分割的多智能体系统，通过模拟人类专家的"全局观察+局部编辑"工作流程，结合三个协作智能体和基于3D U-Net的TopoRefineNet工具，在多轮推理和体素级编辑中实现更准确的拓扑分割，在BigNeuron、CWMBS和ZBFWB数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D神经元分割方法难以同时保留局部细节和全局拓扑结构，导致分割结果碎片化。神经元稀疏细长的形态特征对现有分割方法构成重大挑战。

Method: NeuroRefiner由三个协作智能体组成，分别负责诊断拓扑错误、生成修正指令和验证细化质量。同时提出TopoRefineNet，一种基于3D U-Net的工具，利用跨模态特征融合生成细化掩膜，通过多轮智能体推理和体素级编辑实现分割细化。

Result: 在BigNeuron、CWMBS和ZBFWB三个数据集上的实验表明，NeuroRefiner优于现有最先进方法，在具有挑战性的ZBFWB数据集上F1分数提升了3.02%。

Conclusion: NeuroRefiner通过形式化人类专家工作流程，实现了拓扑更准确且可解释性更强的神经元分割结果，验证了多智能体方法在医学图像分割领域的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate 3D neuron segmentation in fluorescence microscopy is critical for neuroscience. However, the sparse and elongated morphology of neurons poses significant challenges to existing segmentation methods. These methods struggle to preserve both local details and global topology, leading to fragmented results. To address this, we propose NeuroRefiner, a multi-agent system that formalizes the human expert workflow involving iterative global observation and local editing. Specifically, NeuroRefiner comprises three collaborative agents dedicated to diagnosing topological errors, generating correction instructions, and validating refinement quality. To facilitate agent instruction-guided segmentation refinement, we propose TopoRefineNet, a dedicated 3D U-Net-based tool that leverages cross-modality feature fusion to generate refined masks. Through multi-round agent reasoning and voxel-level editing, NeuroRefiner produces topologically more accurate segmentations with enhanced interpretability. Experiments on the BigNeuron, CWMBS, and ZBFWB datasets demonstrate that NeuroRefiner outperforms state-of-the-art methods, notably achieving a 3.02% improvement in F1 score on the challenging ZBFWB dataset.

</details>


### [242] [DUET: A Diversity-Quality Duet of Distillation Experts for Two-Step Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09637)
*Zian Li,Litong Gong,Borui Liao,Pengfei Liu,Xinyu Wang,Xinyuan Wei,Yifan Gao,Tiezheng Ge,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DUET框架，通过噪声级专家分工（sCM负责高噪声步构建多样化结构，DMD负责低噪声步细化外观细节）来调和两步视频生成中质量与多样性之间的权衡，并进一步引入RL引导的专家适配（DUET+）解决中继接口和高噪声阶段瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型实现高质量视频生成但迭代采样成本高，少步蒸馏虽降低开销却面临质量-多样性之间的权衡：轨迹级蒸馏（sCM）偏向多样性，分布级蒸馏（DMD）偏向质量。现有的损失级组合方式存在优化困难，亟需一种能同时兼顾两者的方案。

Method: 提出DUET框架，采用噪声级专家分工：sCM专家负责高噪声步以生成多样化结构，DMD专家负责低噪声步以细化外观细节。两个专家独立训练各自的目标函数，避免损失级组合的优化难题。进一步通过RL引导的专家适配（DUET+）解决中继接口和高噪声阶段的剩余瓶颈。

Result: 基于Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B骨干网络，DUET将两步生成质量提升至接近DMD水平，同时保留约两倍于DMD的结构多样性；DUET+在保持多样性优势的同时进一步提升整体质量。

Conclusion: 噪声级专家专业化是调和两步视频生成中多样性与质量权衡的一种简单有效的范式，为少步扩散视频生成提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: Diffusion models have enabled high-quality video generation in recent years, but the high cost of iterative sampling hinders their practical deployment. Few-step distillation alleviates this cost, yet exposes a quality--diversity trade-off between its two dominant paradigms: trajectory-level distillation (e.g., sCM) favors diversity, whereas distribution-level distillation (e.g., DMD) favors quality. Targeting extreme two-step video generation, we introduce DUET, which reconciles the two paradigms through a noise-level duet of experts: an sCM expert takes the high-noise step to lay out diverse structure, and a DMD expert takes the low-noise step to refine appearance detail. Since the two experts are trained independently with their native objectives, DUET sidesteps the optimization difficulties of loss-level combinations and delivers quality and diversity jointly rather than trading one for the other. We further identify the relay interface and the high-noise stage as the remaining bottlenecks, and address them with RL-guided expert adaptation, yielding DUET+. With the Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B backbone, DUET lifts the two-step quality of sCM close to the level of DMD while retaining nearly all of its structural diversity---about twice that of DMD---and DUET+ further improves overall quality while preserving this diversity advantage. Together, these results establish noise-level expert specialization as a simple, effective paradigm for reconciling diversity and quality in two-step video generation.

</details>


### [243] [EgoHieraLoc: A Cortically Inspired Hierarchical Segmentation-Guided Framework for Egocentric Visual Query Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09656)
*Yifei Cao,Guolong Wang,Mingliang Hou,Xiya Bu,Daming Liu,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoHieraLoc，一个统一框架解决第一人称视频中的视觉查询定位（VQL）问题，同时支持2D和3D定位。该框架受人类视觉层级处理机制启发，包含判别解析模块（利用分割先验提取前景感知查询表示）、查询感知模块（通过判别相关滤波和可变形建模进行鲁棒目标定位）以及区域适应模块（将多尺度上下文反馈到局部区域以恢复精确物体边界）。针对3D定位，提出几何-语义联合置信度（GSJC），将分割置信度与局部深度一致性、多视角反投影一致性和三角化基线质量相乘耦合，确保视角在语义和几何上都可信时才参与3D估计。实验在VQL-2D和VQL-3D基准上均达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉查询定位在物体边界模糊且全局上下文无法有效指导细粒度定位时仍具挑战性。现有方法难以处理前景候选筛选、干扰物下的目标选择和跨视角证据整合等问题。

Method: 提出EgoHieraLoc统一框架，包含三个核心模块：判别解析模块利用分割先验获取前景感知表示；查询感知模块通过判别相关滤波与可变形建模进行目标定位；区域适应模块将多尺度上下文反馈至局部区域恢复边界。对3D定位提出几何-语义联合置信度（GSJC），将分割置信度与多种几何线索（深度一致性、反投影一致性、三角化基线质量）相乘耦合。

Result: 在VQL-2D和VQL-3D基准上均取得了最先进的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过模拟人类层级感知过程（筛选-选择-反馈-跨视角整合），EgoHieraLoc有效解决了物体边界模糊和目标受干扰物影响等VQL难题，并在2D与3D定位任务上均展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Visual query localization (VQL) aims to retrieve and re-localize a queried object in egocentric videos, yet remains challenging when object boundaries are ambiguous and global context cannot effectively guide fine-grained localization. Human vision handles such ambiguity through a hierarchical process: it rapidly screens foreground candidates, selectively attends to the target despite distractors, refines perception via feedback between global context and local detail, and, when a single view is unreliable, integrates evidence across viewpoints according to its credibility. Inspired by these competencies, we propose \textbf{EgoHieraLoc}, a unified framework for VQL-2D and VQL-3D. A Discriminative Parsing Module first extracts foreground-aware query representations using segmentation priors; a Query-Aware Module then performs robust target localization through discriminative correlation filtering with deformable modeling; and a Regional Adaptation Module feeds multi-scale context back into local regions to recover precise object boundaries. To extend this perceptual hierarchy to 3D localization, we introduce Geometric-Semantic Joint Confidence (GSJC), which multiplicatively couples segmentation confidence with local depth consistency, multi-view back-projection consistency, and triangulation-baseline quality, so that a viewpoint contributes to the 3D estimate only when it is credible both semantically and geometrically. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on both VQL-2D and -3D benchmarks.

</details>


### [244] [CIFA: Contextual-Intersectional Fairness Auditing for Hidden Subgroup Discovery in Face Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09669)
*Nazia Aslam,Khalid Adnan Alsayed,Thomas B. Moeslund,Kamal Nasrollahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CIFA框架，用于审计计算机视觉模型中人口统计属性与上下文属性（如光照、模糊、图像质量等）交互作用导致的隐藏子群性能退化问题，并在人脸性别分类任务上验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公平性评估仅关注聚合准确率和人口统计子群分析，忽略了上下文因素与人口统计属性的交互作用，这些交互可能产生隐藏子群，导致性能显著下降，即使整体准确率和人口统计公平性看似可接受。

Method: 提出Contextual-Intersectional Fairness Auditing Framework (CIFA)，包括三个步骤：人口统计审计、上下文审计和上下文交叉审计，随后进行最差组发现（worst-group discovery）以识别和排序最脆弱的属性组合，并采用审计-缓解-再审计（audit-mitigate-reaudit）协议评估既有缓解策略。

Result: 在FairFace、CelebA和UTKFace数据集上使用ResNet-50和ViT-B/16进行评估，发现聚合准确率和仅人口统计评估确实掩盖了显著的上下文交叉差异；既有的缓解策略虽能减少部分最差组差异，但没有单一策略能在所有数据集和架构上一致地消除这些差异。

Conclusion: 上下文交叉审计应作为公平性评估的重要组成部分，CIFA提供了一个可复现的框架，用于发现、优先排序和重新评估人脸分析系统中的隐藏子群风险。

Abstract: Fairness evaluation in computer vision commonly relies on aggregate accuracy and demographic subgroup analysis. However, visual models are also sensitive to contextual factors such as illumination, blur, image quality, facial accessories, and appearance attributes. These factors may interact with demographic characteristics, producing hidden subgroups in which performance degrades substantially despite strong aggregate accuracy and apparently acceptable demographic fairness. To address this, we propose the Contextual-Intersectional Fairness Auditing Framework (CIFA), a structured framework for identifying subgroup vulnerabilities arising from interactions between demographic and contextual attributes. CIFA performs demographic, contextual, and contextual-intersectional auditing, followed by worst-group discovery to identify and rank the most vulnerable attribute combinations. We evaluate CIFA on gender classification using ResNet-50 \cite{he2016deep} and ViT-B/16 \cite{dosovitskiy2020image} across FairFace \cite{Karkkainen2021}, CelebA \cite{Liu2015}, and UTKFace \cite{Zhang2017}. Our results show that aggregate accuracy and demographic-only evaluation can mask substantial contextual-intersectional disparities. We further assess several established mitigation strategies through an audit--mitigate--reaudit protocol and find that, although some worst-group disparities are reduced, no single strategy consistently eliminates them across datasets and architectures. These findings establish contextual-intersectional auditing as an important component of fairness evaluation and provide a reproducible framework for discovering, prioritizing, and reassessing hidden subgroup risks in face analysis systems.

</details>


### [245] [MPISuperRes-PnP: A Super-Resolution Zero-Shot Plug-and-Play Reconstruction Algorithm for Magnetic Particle Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09672)
*Vladyslav Gapyak,Thomas März,Andreas Weinmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于能量最小化的磁粒子成像（MPI）超分辨率重建方法，采用plug-and-play框架，利用预训练的高斯去噪器以零样本方式嵌入深度学习的优势，无需训练数据和避免幻觉伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPI成像中基于测量重建的空间网格非常粗糙，需要超分辨率技术。现有方法缺乏训练数据（MPI训练数据稀缺），且需要避免深度学习训练带来的数据依赖问题。

Method: 将超分辨率纳入能量最小化重建框架，采用plug-and-play的变量分裂方案，从能量最小化推导出分裂算法，其中高斯去噪任务用预训练的深度去噪器以零样本方式处理，无需额外训练。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据（MPIData: EquilibriumModelWithAnisotropy 和 2D-OpenMPI Data）上验证了方法的可行性，未观察到幻觉伪影，去噪器行为保守稳健。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需训练数据的MPI超分辨率方法，该方法通用性强，可扩展至不同正则化器或不同成像任务。

Abstract: Magnetic Particle Imaging (MPI) is an emerging medical imaging modality. MPI is based on the non-linear response of magnetic nanoparticles to an applied magnetic field and avoids ionizing radiation. The measured signal is the voltage induced in receive coils by the particles' response. Reconstructing the particle concentration from the signal constitutes the imaging task. Even using state-of-the-art measurement-based reconstruction, the associated spatial grid is very coarse, hence super-resolution (SR) techniques are important. In this work, we propose an approach for SR in MPI inspired by energy minimization. Different methods have been proposed for SR in MPI, ranging from upscaling of the associated system matrix to interpolation of the reconstruction. Here we incorporate SR into the reconstruction task via an energy minimization formulation. Following the plug-and-play approach to energy minimization we derive a splitting scheme and a SR method for MPI where the arising Gaussian denoising task is treated with a pre-trained learned Gaussian denoiser in a zero-shot fashion. This way, we incorporate benefits of deep learning without training and avoid the need of training data. Further, we provide a quantitative and qualitative evaluation of the proposed method. Hyper-parameter are selected via an extended parameter search. The found parameters are applied for reconstruction on real data. We show the applicability of our method on synthetic and on real data (MPIData: EquilibriumModelWithAnisotropy and 2D-OpenMPI Data). The proposed method employs a deep-learning denoiser without training -- thus it does not require presently scarcely available MPI training data. The denoiser behaves conservatively, i.e., no hallucination artifacts were observed. The SR approach is generic such that it can be applied in future MPI contexts involving different regularizers or different imaging tasks.

</details>


### [246] [Thinking With Tools, Not With Pixels: Tool Calls as Text Scaffolds for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09682)
*Jiahao Shao,Yuanbo Yang,Yiyi Liao,Yujun Shen,Ceyuan Yang,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TextCall方法，通过"只调用工具但不返回图像"来验证一个假设：在当前的think-with-images视觉语言模型中，真正的负载信号是工具调用时生成的文本脚手架（工具名、坐标、目标描述、意图），而非返回的图像像素。实验表明TextCall在准确率上匹配或超越完整视觉思维方法，同时减少29-46%延迟并消除工具执行API调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，工具增强的视觉语言模型在"用图像思考"时，返回的图像像素对模型贡献甚微（通过盲测、增益分解和注意力分析证实）。这引发疑问：如果像素不携带增益，那真正起作用的信号是什么？作者假设是工具调用时发出、在返回像素之前的结构化文本。

Method: 提出TextCall（call-but-no-return）方法：保留工具调用时生成的文本脚手架（工具名、坐标、目标描述、意图），但将返回的图像替换为文本占位符[Image output skipped]。通过三项研究验证：(i)在LoRA、全微调和RL下测试返回像素的非必要性；(ii)在匹配的训练查询上测试脚手架的充分性；(iii)将脚手架分解为推理文本和空间代码，测试各组件贡献度。

Result: (i)在LoRA、全微调和RL设置下，TextCall匹配或超越完整think-with-images方法；在RL下保留了工具使用行为，避免了看到返回图像导致模型停止调用工具的现象。(ii)脚手架输入在匹配查询上产生与返回图像输入相当的准确率。(iii)推理文本和空间代码均有贡献，主导组件因任务而异。TextCall减少29-46%延迟并消除工具执行API调用。

Conclusion: 支持"工具调用脚手架假说"：在当前think-with-images的分布中，活动的信号是工具调用时发出的结构化文本，返回图像是冗余载体。但在当前基准之外，构建像素真正负载的任务仍是未来开放方向。

Abstract: Tool-augmented vision-language models increasingly "think with images": they call crop, zoom, or code tools and reason over the returned pixels. However, recent work using blind tests, gain decompositions, and attention analyses has shown that returned images contribute little, raising the question: if pixels do not carry the gain, what does? We hypothesize that the load-bearing signal is the structured text emitted before any returned pixel arrives: tool name, coordinates, target description, and intent. This textual scaffold encodes where to look and what to find. We introduce TextCall (call-but-no-return) to test this: it keeps the scaffold but replaces returned images with the text placeholder [Image output skipped]. Three studies support the hypothesis. (i) Non-necessity of returned pixels: across LoRA, full fine-tuning, and RL, TextCall matches or exceeds full thinking-with-images; under RL it preserves tool use at the reported checkpoint, avoiding the failure mode where, under matched settings, seeing the returned image causes the model to stop calling tools and answer directly. (ii) Sufficiency of the scaffold: on matched training queries, scaffold-only input yields equivalent accuracy to returned-image input. (iii) Component specificity: decomposing the scaffold into reasoning text and spatial code shows both components contribute, with the dominant one varying by task. Together these results support the Tool-Call Scaffold Hypothesis: in current thinking-with-images distributions, the active signal is the structured text emitted at tool-call time; the returned image is a redundant carrier. TextCall preserves accuracy while reducing latency by 29-46% and eliminating tool-execution API calls. Our claims hold for current thinking-with-images benchmarks; constructing tasks where pixels are genuinely load-bearing remains an open direction.

</details>


### [247] [Diffuse the object, keep its label: curating detector training data from a few unlabeled photographs via VLM-built 3D vegetation scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09691)
*Mario Malizia,Marnix Enting,Rob Haelterman,Ken Hasselmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督站点自适应方法：利用视觉-语言模型从部署站的少量未标注照片中合成带标签的训练图像，通过3D场景构建、目标网格放置和扩散模型重纹理化生成标注数据，使检测器在跨站点目标检测上达到或超过传统跨站点标签复用方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植被中隐蔽小目标的标注图像稀缺，且基于这些图像训练的检测器在不同站点间泛化能力差。传统做法是复用其他站点的标签，但效果不佳。作者希望利用部署站点本身的一小撮未标注照片来合成带标签的训练数据，实现无需人工标注的无监督站点自适应。

Method: 方法包含四个关键步骤：(1) 利用视觉-语言模型从单张照片生成粗粒度3D植被场景；(2) 在场景中放置3D目标网格，直接从场景几何获取边界框、分割掩码和逐实例遮挡信息；(3) 用轻量适配器（在站点照片上微调）对渲染结果进行扩散重纹理化；(4) 引入分级掩码锁定机制，控制扩散过程对目标本身的修改程度，其中轻度扩散目标像素能提升少数类召回率。

Result: 在人道主义扫雷基准上，使用合成图像训练的检测器在多个随机种子上一致地达到或超过使用更大规模真实标注数据（来自其他站点）训练的检测器。消融研究表明，掩码锁定等级是最具影响力的策展选择，且性能提升对照片和裁剪预算不敏感，域内精度无法预测跨站点表现。

Conclusion: 从少量部署站点照片合成训练数据是一种有效的无监督站点自适应策略，能够替代传统的跨站点标签复用，且尤其适合标注稀缺的小目标检测场景。

Abstract: Labeled images of small objects hidden in vegetation are scarce, and detectors trained on them generalize poorly across sites. Rather than reusing labels collected at another site, we synthesize labeled training images from a handful of unlabeled photographs of the deployment site itself. A vision--language model generates a coarse 3D vegetation scene from one photograph; placing 3D object meshes in the scene yields bounding boxes, segmentation masks, and per-instance occlusion directly from the scene geometry, without manual annotation. A lightweight adapter fine-tuned on the photographs conditions a diffusion pass that re-textures the renders, and a graded mask-lock sets how much diffusion may touch the object itself. In our runs this grade was the most influential curation choice: lightly diffusing the object improves minority-class recall over fully protecting its pixels, while unrestricted diffusion dissolves it. Trained on these images, a standard detector matched or exceeded its counterpart trained on a larger labeled dataset of real images from a different site, consistently across seeds on a humanitarian-demining benchmark; the comparison is thus unsupervised site adaptation from a handful of photographs against conventional cross-site label reuse. In our ablations the gains were largely insensitive to the photograph and crop budgets, and in-domain accuracy did not predict cross-site performance.

</details>


### [248] [LookAgain: Closed-Loop GUI Grounding with Visually Grounded Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09723)
*Renshan Zhang,Haoyang Meng,Yixiao He,Rui Shao,April Hua Liu,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LookAgain，一种闭环GUI定位（grounding）方法，将坐标预测视为可反思和修订的假设，通过"定位-回看-修正"的多轮流程（locate/confirm两种原语），结合SFT冷启动和GRPO强化训练，在拒绝感知和通用GUI定位基准上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI grounder在单次预测上表现优异，但在小目标、密集控件和分布外界面上性能急剧下降。作者将这一差距归因于现有方法的范式性局限：没有任何方法将产生的坐标视为待反思和修订的假设，导致三个耦合问题：缺乏事后反思、视觉证据与预测解耦、迭代优化的是视图而非预测本身。

Method: LookAgain将GUI grounding重构为多轮"预测-回看-修正"的闭环过程，包含两个原语：1) "locate"：发布坐标假设，在图像上渲染标记并附加预测区域的局部patch，将前一次预测作为空间先验锚定下一步推理；2) "confirm"：接受或拒绝假设并终止流程。训练分两阶段：先用SFT在构造的反思轨迹上进行冷启动，再使用GRPO，以终端grounding正确性作为唯一奖励。

Result: 在拒绝感知和通用GUI grounding基准上，LookAgain一致地提升了性能并达到最先进水平。全面的消融实验验证了所提出框架各组的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将坐标预测从一次性输出转变为可验证、可修订的闭环过程，LookAgain有效解决了现有GUI grounder在困难场景下性能退化的根本性问题，为GUI grounding提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Recent graphical user interface (GUI) grounders have significantly advanced single-shot accuracy on standard benchmarks, yet their performance degrades sharply on small targets, densely packed controls and out-of-distribution interfaces. We attribute this gap to a paradigmatic limitation shared by existing approaches: none of them treats a produced coordinate as a hypothesis to be reflected upon and revised under new visual evidence. This manifests as three coupled issues: 1) Lack of post-hoc reflection. The prediction is frozen at the moment of emission, leaving no internal mechanism to challenge or refine it. 2) Visual evidence decoupled from the prediction. The auxiliary visual evidence is gathered to support the upcoming coordinate rather than to scrutinise the one already committed to. 3) Refinement over views, not over predictions. The iterative zoom-in refines the inspected region instead of inheriting a previous coordinate as a spatial prior to be corrected. In this paper, we propose LookAgain, a closed-loop GUI grounder driven by post-prediction visual reflection. LookAgain reformulates grounding as a multi-turn predict-look-again-refine process with two primitives: "locate" posts a coordinate hypothesis, renders a marker on the image and appends a local patch of the predicted region. It anchors the next reasoning step to the previous prediction as a spatial prior; "confirm" accepts or reject the hypothesis and terminates the procedure. We train the LookAgain grounder with SFT on constructed reflective trajectories as a cold start, followed by GRPO with terminal grounding correctness as the sole reward. Extensive experiments show that LookAgain consistently improves performance on both refusal-aware and general GUI grounding benchmarks, achieving state-of-the-art results. Comprehensive ablations further verify the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [249] [World Tokens: Enhancing Embodied Policies with Training-Time World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09730)
*Qu Tang,Benhui Zhuang,Bo Yuan,Xue Yu,Longteng Guo,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出World Tokens，一种以World Adapter为核心的具身策略架构，在训练时利用世界模型增强动作策略，部署时移除视频模型分支，保持VLA级别的推理延迟，在LIBERO、SIMPLER和真实机器人R1 Pro上均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型擅长高效闭环控制但缺乏对物理场景演化的显式建模；而世界动作模型（WAMs）虽能捕捉时空演化，但保留未来视频生成或大型视频骨干网络会显著增加推理成本，需要在世界建模能力与部署效率之间寻求平衡。

Method: World Adapter将VLM特征转换为固定数量的世界token，这些token同时条件化联合微调的未来视频去噪器，并作为动作专家的唯一视觉-语言上下文。通过共享条件化，未来视频去噪的梯度直接塑造用于动作预测的表征；通过独占路由防止策略绕过该表征。部署时仅保留VLM、World Adapter和动作专家，移除世界模型分支。

Result: 在2B骨干且无具身动作预训练的条件下，World Tokens在LIBERO上极具竞争力，在SIMPLER上取得最佳平均成绩，在真实R1 Pro机器人上显著优于匹配的动作基线，且动作块生成延迟达到VLA水平。

Conclusion: World Tokens通过训练时世界建模增强策略表征、部署时零额外视频推理开销的设计，成功兼顾了世界动力学建模能力与高效部署，证明了无需在线视频模型即可获得世界模型带来的策略性能提升。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are a widely adopted paradigm for embodied policies. They excel at efficient closed-loop control but do not explicitly model how physical scenes evolve as a task unfolds. Recently emerging world-action models (WAMs) leverage pretrained video world models to capture spatiotemporal evolution, yet retaining future generation or a large video backbone in the control loop substantially increases inference cost. We introduce World Tokens, an embodied policy architecture built around a World Adapter that bridges visual-language understanding, world-dynamics modeling, and action generation. It uses world modeling during training to enhance the action policy while preserving efficient deployment. Specifically, the World Adapter transforms VLM features into a fixed set of world tokens, which condition a jointly fine-tuned future-video denoiser and simultaneously serve as the action expert's sole visual-language context. This shared conditioning allows gradients from future-video denoising to directly shape the representation used for action prediction, while exclusive routing prevents the policy from bypassing that representation. At deployment, the world-model branch is removed, leaving only the VLM, World Adapter, and action expert, with no online video-model inference. With a 2B backbone and no embodied action pretraining, World Tokens is highly competitive on LIBERO, attains the best reported averages on SIMPLER, substantially improves real-world R1 Pro success over a matched action-only baseline, and generates each action chunk at VLA-level latency.

</details>


### [250] [HandSplatter: Automated Digital Goniometry from Neural Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09735)
*Emmett Chen,Neal Chen,Xiang Li,Quanzheng Li,Siyeop Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经渲染的3D手部关节定位与姿态估计新流程，结合2D特征提取与视图合成，并引入离散密度爬山算法进行3D空间地标修正，以提高手指关节活动度（ROM）测量的精度和临床可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手和手指疾病是肌肉骨骼致残的主要原因，需要精确的关节运动量化方法。当前标准工具量角器存在劳动强度大、评分者间可靠性不一致的问题；现有数字替代方案又缺乏临床所需的精度，因此需要一种更准确、更客观的ROM测量方法。

Method: 提出一种新颖的3D手部关节定位与姿态估计流程，核心包括：(1) 结合2D特征提取与神经渲染的视图合成技术来提升精度；(2) 引入离散密度爬山算法（discrete density hill climbing）在3D空间中对投影地标进行有意义的修正。

Result: 该方法克服了手动测量的低效率以及现有软件精度不足的问题，显著提升了关节定位的准确性和临床可行性，为客观功能评估提供了稳健的工具。

Conclusion: 该神经渲染驱动的管线为手指关节ROM测量提供了一种高精度、可重复且客观的临床评估方案，有望替代传统量角器作为诊断和康复监测的标准工具。

Abstract: Hand and finger disorders are leading contributors to musculoskeletal disability, creating a clinical need for precise methods to quantify joint motion. Range of motion (ROM) serves as the metric for diagnosis, rehabilitation monitoring, and evaluating surgical outcomes. Currently, the goniometer is the standard tool for assessing finger flexion and extension. However, manual goniometry is labor-intensive and suffers from inconsistent inter-rater reliability due to variations in examiner technique. While digital alternatives exist, current software-based approaches often lack the necessary accuracy for clinical usage. To address these limitations, we present a novel pipeline for 3-D hand joint location and pose estimation using neural rendering. Unlike previous methods, our approach combines 2-D feature extraction with view synthesis to significantly improve accuracy and clinical viability. Furthermore, we introduce a discrete density hill climbing algorithm that facilitates the meaningful correction of projected landmarks in 3-D space. This system overcomes the inefficiencies of manual measurement and the inaccuracies of existing software, providing a robust tool for objective functional assessment.

</details>


### [251] [Disentangling Co-Occurring Retinal Pathologies with Saliency-Guided Sparse Expert Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09752)
*Nagur Shareef Shaik,Jeongwoo Park,Yeong-Jin Kim,Jaeuk Jung,Hyunjung Oh,Dong Hye Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于稀疏条件计算的视网膜眼底图像多病种分类架构，结合引导上下文门控（GCG）空间注意力前端和稀疏路由的混合专家（MoE）块，实现了可解释的、疾病相关的专家分配，在五类基准上达到0.912±0.008 macro AUC和0.653±0.014 macro F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视网膜眼底图像常同时存在多种病变，但标准深度学习分类器对所有图像应用静态、相同的计算，未考虑潜在疾病分布的差异性，缺乏对复杂多病种场景的自适应处理能力。

Method: 提出新架构：1) 引导上下文门控（GCG）空间注意力前端用于特征筛选；2) 基于特征令牌的稀疏路由混合专家（MoE）块进行条件计算；3) 通过专家路由实现可解释的数据驱动分解，使不同疾病形态分配到专属专家。

Result: 专家分配显著依赖疾病类别（p<0.001），正常状态和形态独特的病理（如ERM、AMD）各自隔离到专属专家。在患者隔离的五折交叉验证中，模型达到0.912±0.008 macro AUC和0.653±0.014 macro F1。Grad-CAM++和后MoE t-SNE可视化证实专家路由与局部病灶对齐，并在几何上将共发病例映射到其组成簇之间。

Conclusion: 稀疏MoE可作为多病种视网膜筛查的可解释方法，其专家路由不仅能提升分类性能，还能揭示病理间的结构关系，为临床可解释的AI辅助诊断提供了新方向。

Abstract: Retinal fundus images frequently exhibit multiple co-occurring pathologies, yet standard deep learning classifiers apply static, identical computation to every image regardless of the underlying disease distribution. We propose a novel architecture that resolves this via sparse conditional computation, pairing a Guided Context Gating (GCG) spatial attention front-end with a sparsely-routed Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) block operating over feature tokens. Crucially, this routing yields an interpretable, data-driven decomposition. Expert allocation is significantly disease-dependent (p < 0.001), with the healthy Normal state and morphologically distinct pathologies (e.g., ERM, AMD) isolating to dedicated experts. On a five-class, patient-disjoint 5-fold cross-validation benchmark, our model achieves 0.912 +/- 0.008 macro AUC and 0.653 +/- 0.014 macro F1. Furthermore, Grad-CAM++ and post-MoE t-SNE visualizations confirm that expert routing aligns with localized lesions and geometrically maps co-occurring cases between their constituent clusters, positioning sparse MoE as an interpretable approach to multi-disease retinal screening.

</details>


### [252] [C$^2$A: Coupling Spatial Evidence with Clinical Priors via Co-occurrence Aware Class Attention for Multi-Label Chest X-Ray Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09774)
*Akash Gogineni,Nagur Shareef Shaik,Aasrith Mandava,Adnan Masood,Dong Hye Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出C²A（共现感知类注意力）分类头，通过将池化转化为学习到的逐类空间注意力图的期望来获得局部化描述符，并利用从经验标签共现中热启动的可学习图来耦合这些描述符，在CheXpert上达到0.895的宏平均AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部病理很少孤立出现，但标准多标签分类器依赖共享的全局描述符，忽略了发现的位置信息以及它们如何共现，这限制了分类性能。

Method: C²A首先将池化建模为对学习到的逐类空间注意力图的期望，为每种疾病生成局部化描述符；然后通过从经验标签共现中热启动的可学习图耦合这些描述符，使用单次残差消息传递步骤在相关发现间共享证据，其中共现通过显式双线性交互进入每个logit，且该操作被证明是恒等映射的有界扰动。

Result: 在CheXpert上，C²A达到0.895的宏平均AUROC，优于先进的上下文门控基线。增益集中在具有模糊空间证据的高共现类别上（如Atelectasis比GCG提升+1.5），且仅引入一条线性投影和C×C边矩阵的极低开销。

Conclusion: C²A通过显式耦合空间证据与临床共现先验，以极小的计算开销有效提升了多标签胸部疾病分类性能，尤其对空间证据模糊的高共现类别效果显著。

Abstract: Thoracic pathologies rarely occur in isolation, yet standard multi-label classifiers rely on shared global descriptors, discarding \emph{where} findings lie and \emph{how} they co-occur. We propose \textbf{C$\mathbf{^2}$A} (Co-occurrence Aware Class Attention), a classification head that explicitly couples spatial evidence with clinical priors. First, C$^2$A casts pooling as an expectation over learned per-class spatial attention maps, yielding localized descriptors for each disease. Second, it couples these descriptors via a learnable graph warm-started from empirical label co-occurrence. A single residual message-passing step shares evidence among related findings, proving to be a bounded perturbation of the identity where co-occurrence enters each logit through an explicit bilinear interaction. On CheXpert, C$^2$A achieves a superior $0.895$ macro-mean AUROC, outperforming advanced context-gating baselines. Crucially, gains concentrate on highly co-occurrent classes with ambiguous spatial evidence (rescuing Atelectasis by $+1.5$ over GCG), demonstrating the prior's regularizing effect with a negligible overhead of one linear projection and a $C\!\times\!C$ edge matrix.

</details>


### [253] [NTIRE 2026 Low-light Enhancement: Twilight Cowboy Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09782)
*Aleksei Khalin,Egor Ershov,Artyom Panshin,Sergey Korchagin,Georgiy Lobarev,Arseniy Terekhin,Sofiia Dorogova,Amir Shamsutdinov,Yasin Mamedov,Bakhtiyar Khalfin,Bogdan Sheludko,Emil Zilyaev,Nikola Banić,Georgy Perevozchikov,Radu Timofte,Shuai Liu,Yuqian Zhang,Lize Zhang,Yibin Huang,Chaoyu Feng,Luyang Wang,Xiaotao Wang,Dongqing Zou,Lei Lei,Tianli Liu,Dejun Hao,Chunxia Lei,Furkan Kınlı,Andrei Mironov,Alexander Dikov,Aleksei Sadokhin,Vladimir Zvorygin,Constantine Habarlak,Shuwei Yue,Egor Mirantsov,Daniil Okunev,Dmitry Arkhipov,Aleksandr Yugay,Anas M. Ali,Bilel Benjdira,Wadii Boulila,Wei Zhou,Linfeng Li,Lingdong Kong,Jiachen Tu,Guoyi Xu,Yaoxin Jiang,Jiajia Liu,Yaokun Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文回顾了NTIRE 2026低光增强挑战赛（Twilight Cowboy），该挑战旨在将低光条件下拍摄的多张未对齐智能手机RAW图像融合为一张干净图像，同时解决视觉退化（噪声、混合光源）和运动导致的几何不一致问题。比赛采用585个真实场景数据集，通过三阶段评估协议（CodaBench自动验证+盲测），十支队伍超过基线，PSNR最高提升+6.49 dB，SSIM提升+0.0101，创下突发低光图像增强的新纪录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光摄影同时面临两大挑战：高噪声和混合光源导致的视觉退化，以及手持拍摄多帧时运动引起的几何不一致。现有方法往往分别处理这两个问题，缺乏统一解决方案的基准测试平台。

Method: 构建包含585个真实室内低光和室外夜间场景的数据集；采用三阶段评估协议：第一、二阶段通过CodaBench平台自动验证，第三阶段在私有测试集上进行盲评以确定最终排名；参赛方案需在RAW域融合多张未对齐图像。

Result: 十支参赛队伍超越基线，PSNR最高提升+6.49 dB，SSIM提升+0.0101，建立了突发式低光图像增强的新state-of-the-art性能，证明在真实噪声、运动和光照变化环境下取得了显著进展。

Conclusion: 该挑战展示了RAW域多帧融合方法在低光增强中的巨大潜力，为低光和夜间计算摄影研究提供了公开基准和数据集，推动该领域持续进步。

Abstract: This paper presents a review of the NTIRE 2026 Low-light Enhancement: Twilight Cowboy Challenge. The objective of the competition was to merge a set of misaligned smartphone images in the raw domain, captured in low-light conditions, into a single, clean image. Introduced setup simultaneously addresses two problems of low-light photography: visual degradations such as high noise and mixed scene illuminants, and the geometric inconsistencies caused by hand movement during multi-frame capture. To advance research in low-light and nighttime computational photography, a challenging dataset was collected comprising 585 real-world scenes, spanning indoor low-light and outdoor nighttime conditions, for training and benchmarking participant solutions. The competition employed a three-stage evaluation protocol: automatic validation via the CodaBench platform in stages one and two, followed by blind assessment on a private test set for the final ranking. Ten teams surpassed the established baseline, achieving improvements of up to +6.49 dB in PSNR and +0.0101 in SSIM, thereby establishing new state-of-the-art performance for burst-based low-light image enhancement. These results demonstrate significant progress in handling real-world noise, motion, and illumination variability in the low-light setting. Comprehensive results, leaderboards, and additional information are publicly available at https://nightimaging.org.

</details>


### [254] [ADOPD: Reference-Privileged On-Policy Distillation for MLLM-Based Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09789)
*Jingtai He,Shiyuan Meng,Wenchao Meng,Qinmin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ADOPD是一种参考特权在线策略蒸馏框架，使仅查询图像的多模态大语言模型在工业异常检测中无需推理时检索参考图像即可达到甚至超越单样本参考比较的性能，在MMAD基准上零样本推理平均准确率达77.31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通过推理时比较查询图像与参考图像可提升工业异常检测精度，但这依赖额外的检索和处理成本。作者研究是否可以将参考比较带来的收益内化到模型参数中，从而在推理时无需检索参考图像也能获得同等性能。

Method: 提出ADOPD（参考特权在线策略蒸馏）框架：训练阶段由能访问参考图像的教师模型监督仅查询图像的学生模型。教师评估学生生成的rollout在匹配和不匹配参考下的表现；匹配参考与不匹配参考的教师-学生对数比值定义token级学习方向；两种参考视图之间的似然差距估计参考特定支持度并校准序列级权重。

Result: 在MMAD基准上零样本推理平均准确率达77.31%，相比Qwen3-VL-4B骨干网络提升6.14个点，并超越其单样本设置2.64个点。

Conclusion: ADOPD能够从参考比较中学习到细粒度的异常检测策略，将参考信息内化到模型参数中，在零样本推理条件下达到甚至超过显式参考比较的性能，验证了在线策略蒸馏框架在IAD任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection (IAD) requires identifying fine-grained deviations from normal visual patterns. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can improve recognition accuracy by comparing query images with references at inference time, but these benefits rely on additional retrieval and processing. We investigate whether the benefits of reference comparison can instead be internalized in the model parameters. Access to references during training allows a reference-aware teacher to supervise a query-only student. However, the teacher may favor plausible responses based on query cues or language priors rather than valid visual information. We propose ADOPD, a reference-privileged on-policy distillation framework. The teacher evaluates student-generated rollouts under matched and mismatched references. The matched-reference teacher-to-student log-ratio defines the token-level learning direction, specifying what the student should learn. The likelihood gap between the two reference views estimates reference-specific support and calibrates the sequence-level weight. ADOPD achieves 77.31% average accuracy on the MMAD benchmark under zero-shot inference, improving the Qwen3-VL-4B backbone by 6.14 points and outperforming its one-shot setting by 2.64 points. Experiments show that ADOPD learns a fine-grained anomaly inspection strategy from reference comparison. The project will be available at https://github.com/withTai/ADOPD.

</details>


### [255] [Modern Backbones Improve Multi-task DETR for Mammography Classification and Lesion Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09801)
*Dinh Tan Nguyen,Quang-Hien Kha,Le-Hoang Nguyen,Minh-Toan Dinh,Xuan-Huy Nguyen,Dac Phu Ho,Cao Truong Tran,Sai Ho Ling,Lan T Ho-Pham,Liem Pham,Nguyen Quoc Khanh Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于多任务DETR框架的乳腺X线摄影AI系统，同时实现图像级恶性预测和病灶定位，并在OPTIMAM和SGM1k两个数据集上比较了多种现代骨干网络，发现ConvNeXtV2在OPTIMAM上表现最佳（AUC 97.96%），DINOv3在SGM1k上表现最佳（AUC 90.97%），表明骨干网络质量是多任务乳腺X线摄影的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将考试级预测与候选区域定位相结合可能提升AI在乳腺X线摄影中的实用性，现有研究缺乏对此多任务设置下不同骨干网络性能的系统评估。

Method: 采用多任务DETR框架，通过共享表征同时支持图像级恶性预测和病灶定位，在OPTIMAM和活检确证的SGM1k队列上评估多种骨干网络（包括ConvNeXtV2、DINOv3、MambaVision及较老的ResNet风格特征）。

Result: 在OPTIMAM上，ConvNeXtV2达到97.96% AUC、99.89%灵敏度、25.08% mAP@.5和74.38% recall@.25；在SGM1k上，DINOv3达到90.97% AUC、86.28%灵敏度、82.00%特异性、27.04% mAP@.5和77.32% recall@.25。现代骨干普遍优于ResNet风格特征，MambaVision竞争力较弱。

Conclusion: 骨干网络质量是影响多任务乳腺X线摄影效果的关键因素，ConvNeXtV2在该框架下是表现突出且具备良好匹配度的CNN骨干网络。

Abstract: Joint exam-level prediction and candidate-region localization may improve the usefulness of AI support in mammography. We study this setting using a multi-task DETR framework, where shared representations support both image-level malignancy prediction and lesion localization, and evaluate its performance on OPTIMAM and a biopsy-confirmed SGM1k cohort. Across both datasets, modern backbones consistently outperformed older ResNet-style features, with ConvNeXtV2 and DINOv3 giving the strongest overall results, whereas MambaVision was less competitive. On OPTIMAM, ConvNeXtV2 achieved the best overall performance, reaching 97.96% AUC, 99.89% sensitivity, 25.08% mAP@.5, and 74.38% recall@.25. On SGM1k, DINOv3 gave the strongest overall results, with 90.97% AUC, 86.28% sensitivity, 82.00% specificity, 27.04% mAP@.5, and 77.32% recall@.25. These findings suggest that backbone quality is a critical factor in effective multi-task mammography, with ConvNeXtV2 emerging as a particularly strong and well-matched CNN backbone for mammography in this framework.

</details>


### [256] [MedPixel: A Unified Pixel-Language Model for Medical Reasoning and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09818)
*Haoyu Yang,Meixing Shi,Zengjie Chen,Haoran Sun,Haitao Leng,Xiaoming Shi,Yuxiang Cai,Yankai Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MedPixel，一个统一的医学像素-语言模型，通过共享语言-掩码接口连接临床语言理解与像素级定位。同时引入MedPLG-440K数据集（约44万像素-语言任务样本）和Pixel-Level Preference Optimization（像素级偏好优化）训练策略，全面支持显式定位、隐式推理、空间交互、地面解释和医学VQA等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型缺乏精确的像素级定位能力，而医学分割模型往往依赖明确的类别或精确的空间提示。这种割裂源于监督信号的不匹配：分割数据集提供精确掩码但缺少语言监督，医学视觉语言数据则很少将语言与密集空间标注配对。

Method: 1) 提出MedPixel统一模型，基于共享语言-掩码接口架构；2) 构建MedPLG-440K数据集，通过临床驱动的合成过程生成约44万像素-语言任务样本，无需外部LLM标注；3) 采用联合多任务监督微调，随后进行Pixel-Level Preference Optimization，利用真实掩码作为离线验证器从掩码质量推导响应偏好。

Result: MedPixel在显式定位、隐式推理、空间交互、地面解释和医学VQA等广泛任务上均取得优异的像素级预测与响应生成性能，并展现出对外部定位基准的有效零样本迁移能力及对不精确空间提示的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MedPixel通过统一语言-掩码接口和可扩展的像素-语言监督策略，有效弥合了医学视觉语言模型与分割模型之间的鸿沟，在广泛的医学任务上实现像素级预测与语言生成的协同性能。

Abstract: Reliable medical image understanding requires models to connect clinical language and visual reasoning with pixel-level grounding. Yet medical vision-language models often lack precise localization, whereas medical segmenters typically rely on explicit target categories or precise spatial prompts. This divide is reinforced by a supervision mismatch: segmentation datasets provide precise masks but little language supervision, whereas medical vision-language data rarely pair language with dense spatial annotations. To address this gap, we present MedPixel, a unified medical pixel-language model built around a shared language--mask interface. To provide scalable supervision, we introduce MedPLG-440K, comprising approximately 440K pixel-language task samples constructed through a clinically motivated synthesis process without external LLM annotation. MedPixel is trained with joint multi-task supervised fine-tuning followed by Pixel-Level Preference Optimization, which uses ground-truth masks as offline verifiers to derive response preferences from mask quality. MedPixel supports a broad spectrum of tasks spanning explicit grounding, implicit reasoning, spatial interaction, grounded explanation, and medical VQA. Across this task spectrum, MedPixel achieves strong performance in both pixel-level prediction and response generation, together with effective zero-shot transfer to external grounding benchmarks and robustness to imperfect spatial prompts. Code and model checkpoints will be released at https://github.com/yhy-whu/Medpixel.

</details>


### [257] [From Diagnosis to Correction: Benchmarking and Improving Real-World Table Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09842)
*Jutao Xiao,Yuan Qu,Dongsheng Ma,Fan Wu,Tianyao He,Weihong Li,Jie Yang,Yu Qiao,Bin Wang,Conghui He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TableParseMap诊断基准（916个真实复杂表格、五类挑战场景、九类失败类型），发现最强解析器仅有85.03 TEDS，并提出了DEC（分解-增强-纠正）智能体框架，通过视觉一致性门控与排序器提升冻结表格解析器性能，平均提升TEDS 1.57分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析器在OmniDocBench等聚合基准上TEDS超过93分，但在真实复杂表格上持续失败，聚合基准得分掩盖了实质性弱点；该差距尚未被系统量化。

Method: 构建TableParseMap诊断基准（916个真实表格、五种挑战场景、九类失败类型）；提出DEC框架，以通用VLM为控制器，包含Decompose（结构感知边界划分大表）、Enhance（增强弱视觉证据并重解析）、Correct（诊断修复残留错误），并设计VC-Gate选择性触发干预和VC-Ranker验证候选更新支持回滚；从4,556个候选表中推导出1,977表的Consensus-Hard Set。

Result: 最强解析器在TableParseMap上仅达85.03 TEDS；DEC在三个冻结解析器上平均提升TEDS 1.57分；在TableParseMap上总体提升1.89分，结构错误上提升2.62分，大表上提升5.66分。

Conclusion: 聚合基准得分掩盖了真实复杂表格解析的重大缺陷；DEC通过视觉一致性引导的智能体框架，无需重新训练即可有效提升冻结解析器的性能，为复杂表格解析提供了可行的改进路径。

Abstract: Recent document parsers achieve table TEDS scores above 93 on OmniDocBench v1.6, yet community feedback and our audit reveal persistent failures on complex real-world tables. To quantify this gap, we introduce TableParseMap, a diagnostic benchmark of 916 real-world tables organized into five challenging scenarios and nine failure types. The strongest evaluated parser achieves only 85.03 TEDS, showing that aggregate benchmark scores conceal substantial weaknesses. Our analysis attributes these failures to three complementary limitations: large tables exceed the reliable processing scale of a single pass, weak or ambiguous visual cues hinder structure perception, and the reconstructed table may remain visually inconsistent with the image. We therefore propose DEC (Decompose--Enhance--Correct), a visual-consistency-guided agentic framework that improves frozen table parsers without retraining. DEC uses a general VLM as the controller: Decompose partitions large tables along structure-aware boundaries, Enhance exposes weak visual evidence and reparses transformed views, and Correct diagnoses and repairs residual errors. A Visual Consistency Gate (VC-Gate) selectively triggers intervention, while a Visual Consistency Ranker (VC-Ranker) verifies candidate updates and supports rollback without ground-truth HTML at inference time. We further derive a 1,977-table Consensus-Hard Set from 4,556 candidates through offline metrics and cross-model consensus. Across three frozen parsers, DEC improves TEDS by 1.57 points on average; on TableParseMap, gains reach 1.89 points overall, 2.62 on structural errors, and 5.66 on large tables.

</details>


### [258] [Sci-VBench: Evaluating Knowledge- and Reasoning-Intensive Video Generation in Science Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09873)
*Diandian Zhang,Tingyu Song,Lin Fu,Zheyuan Yang,Yilun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Sci-VBench，一个面向科学领域的视频生成基准测试，包含1,253个专家标注示例，覆盖四大核心学科60个主题，并建立了基于评分标准的评估协议，对16个前沿模型进行了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成评估多关注表面视觉合理性，缺乏对科学知识和推理能力的评估，需要更全面的基准来测试模型在科学领域的知识密集型和推理密集型视频生成能力。

Method: 构建包含1,253个专家标注示例的Sci-VBench基准，覆盖自然科学、医疗保健、人文社科和工程四大领域60个主题；设计基于评分标准的评估协议，利用非专家人类评估者和多模态大语言模型作为评判系统进行可扩展的评估。

Result: 非专家评估者和MLLM评判系统与专家判断高度一致；基准测试16个模型发现，自动感知质量分数在各系统间聚集紧密，但提示词基础和科学与因果正确性方面差异显著，且专有模型与开源模型之间存在明显差距。

Conclusion: 当前视频生成模型在视觉真实感方面的进步尚未转化为对科学和因果动态的可靠建模，揭示了该领域需要进一步发展的关键方向。

Abstract: We introduce Sci-VBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating knowledge- and reasoning-intensive video generation across scientific domains. It contains 1,253 expert-annotated examples spanning 60 subjects across four core disciplines: Natural Science, Healthcare, Humanities & Social Sciences, and Engineering. Each example requires models to generate temporally rich videos that demand scientific reasoning and knowledge-grounded synthesis, going beyond surface-level visual plausibility. We further establish a rubric-based evaluation protocol. Our analysis shows that, under this protocol, both non-expert human evaluators and MLLM-as-Judge systems can achieve relatively high agreement with expert judgments, supporting reproducible evaluation at scale. We benchmark 16 frontier proprietary and open-source models and find that, while automatic perceptual-quality scores cluster tightly across systems, performance on Prompt Grounding and Scientific and Causal Correctness varies substantially, with a pronounced proprietary-open-source gap. These findings show that advances in visual realism have not yet translated into reliable modeling of scientific and causal dynamics.

</details>


### [259] [Financial Numerical Prediction and Allocation as Token Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09880)
*Xu Ouyang,Moontae Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FinATOM，一种无需任务特定预测头（head-free）的统一框架，让因果语言模型直接通过受限token生成来完成三步股票收益预测和动态五ETF资产配置。在2023-2025年ETF测试中，配置策略将毛/净Sharpe比率分别提升至1.529和1.494；在FinTexTS数据集上取得73.72%的累计收益和2.69的Sharpe。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统金融预测依赖任务特定的回归、排序或策略头部，将语言模型与实际评估的数值目标分离。作者希望探究因果语言模型能否直接通过受限token生成来表示预测和决策，从而建立统一、无头部的金融预测与决策界面。

Method: FinATOM包含两个模型：(1) 预测模型自回归生成波动率标准化收益token，使用序数与排序监督训练，随后进行一个epoch的token级策略微调；(2) 配置模型生成归一化多头权重，通过有监督微调模仿因果均值-方差锚定策略，并使用DAPO增强的GRPO优化21日实际Sharpe同时保持锚定一致性。输入采用多模态形式。

Result: 在2023-2025年ETF测试中，配置策略将池化毛Sharpe从1.428提升至1.529，净Sharpe（5bp交易成本）从1.394提升至1.494；多模态配置输入取得最高三期平均Sharpe为1.540，在2025年优势最明显。在FinTexTS上，SFT和策略方法分别达到73.52%/2.68和73.72%/2.69的累计收益/Sharpe。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，直接通过语言模型token生成进行金融数值预测和决策是可行的，这为统一金融语言模型范式提供了支持，同时作者建议在更广泛的资产类别、市场周期和随机种子下进行进一步测试。

Abstract: Financial prediction typically relies on task-specific regression, ranking, or policy heads, separating the language model from the numerical object ultimately evaluated. We investigate whether a causal language model can instead represent forecasts and decisions directly through constrained token generation. FinATOM introduces a unified, head-free interface for three-step stock-return forecasting and dynamic five-ETF allocation. The forecasting model autoregressively emits volatility-standardized return tokens and is trained with ordinal and ranking supervision followed by a one-epoch token-level policy stage. The allocation model generates normalized long-only weights; supervised fine-tuning imitates a causal mean--variance anchor, and DAPO-augmented GRPO optimizes realized 21-day Sharpe subject to anchor consistency. In 2023--2025 ETF tests, the allocation policy improves pooled gross Sharpe from 1.428 to 1.529 and net Sharpe under a 5-bp transaction-cost model from 1.394 to 1.494. The multimodal allocation input attains the highest three-period mean Sharpe of 1.540, with its clearest advantage in 2025. On FinTexTS, the SFT and policy strategies achieve 73.52\%/2.68 and 73.72\%/2.69 cumulative-return/Sharpe, respectively. These results support the feasibility of direct language-model token generation for financial numerical prediction and decision-making, while motivating broader tests across assets, regimes, and random seeds.

</details>


### [260] [Space-Creating versus Dead Possession: An Off-Ball Possession-Quality Index for Broadcast Football](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09887)
*Seongjin Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双层的"垃圾控球"识别框架：第一层基于事件数据（期望威胁网格重建实时比分）构建垃圾控球指数，识别在平局或落后状态下低威胁的控球序列；第二层通过广播视频投影到球场坐标，计算空间创造指数（SCI），区分空间死亡型与空间创造型控球。在2026世界杯103场比赛上，垃圾控球标志与积分显著负相关（r=-0.37），且在控制VAEP和场地倾斜后依然显著（p<0.0001）。视频分析显示74%的标志窗口确实为非空间创造型。该框架能区分"创造空间但未转化"与"无效控球"，这是纯事件型控球价值方法无法做到的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的事件型控球价值框架（预期威胁、VAEP、控球价值）只对有效触球定价，忽略了无球侧的"死循环控球"问题——持有球权究竟是创造了空间还是仅仅循环传递？最常引用的控球率数字具有误导性（本方半场60%的控球不等于压制对手）。

Method: 两层方法：(1) 事件侧构建垃圾控球指数，通过预期威胁网格为每次控球序列的峰值威胁增益定价，并重建实时比分以排除领先状态下的保护性循环，标记平局或落后状态下的低威胁序列；(2) 对标记窗口，将广播视频投影到球场坐标并计算空间创造指数（SCI），即净控球变化，衡量控球是否占据空间或把对手防线推后。

Result: 在2026世界杯103场（206队次）上，垃圾控球标志与积分负相关（r=-0.37）且与预期进球差负相关（r=-0.51）。固定团队进攻VAEP和场地倾斜后，垃圾标志仍与积分强烈负相关（p<0.0001，按比赛聚类），而VAEP不显著。视频分析中31/35个标记窗口有74%为非空间创造型、19%弱推进、6%为事件标志误判的空间创造型窗口。一个73%控球率却点球出局的球队有两次非创造型控球窗口。

Conclusion: 双层框架有效分离了"空间创造但未转化"与"无效（垃圾）控球"两种情形，这是仅依赖事件型控球价值模型无法区分的。垃圾控球指数提供了超越传统on-ball价值模型（如VAEP）的增量信息，为战术后勤和比赛分析提供了更精细的诊断工具。

Abstract: Ball possession is the most-cited and most-misleading number in football: 60% recycled in one's own half is not 60% spent pinning the opponent back. Existing event-based possession-value frameworks (expected threat, VAEP, on-ball value) price on-ball actions but ignore the off-ball question a sterile possession poses: did holding the ball create space, or was the circulation dead? We answer this in two layers. First, an event-side junk-possession index prices each possession sequence by its peak threat gain under an expected-threat grid and -- after reconstructing the live scoreline to exclude lead-protecting circulation -- flags low-threat sequences in tied-or-losing states. On the 2026 FIFA World Cup (103 matches, 206 team-matches) the flag correlates negatively with points (r=-0.37) and xG difference (r=-0.51, partly index-coupled). It is not a repackaging of on-ball value: with team offensive VAEP and field tilt held fixed, the junk flag stays strongly negatively associated with points (p<0.0001, also match-clustered) while VAEP is not significant -- in this same-match (descriptive) regression it adds information beyond this on-ball action-value model. Second, for a flagged window we resolve whether it was spatially dead or space-creating by projecting broadcast video to pitch coordinates and measuring a Space-Creation Index (SCI): a net pitch-control change capturing whether the possession seized space or pushed the opponent's block back. Across 31 of 35 flagged windows from nine World Cup matches (a purposive sample), 74% are spatially non-space-creating, 19% weak progression, and 6% space-creating windows the event flag alone would score as failure -- including a side with 73% of the ball that exited on penalties (two non-creating windows). The two layers separate space-creating-but-unconverted from sterile possession, a distinction event-only on-ball value cannot make.

</details>


### [261] [DistMoE: Private-data Rehearsal-free Routing in Mixture-of-Experts for Distributed Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09907)
*Mainak Singha,Niccolò Biondi,Elisa Ricci,Subhankar Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DistMoE是一种基于混合专家（MoE）的分布式视觉指令微调方法，通过为每个客户端分配私有FFN专家并结合公共专家进行模块化路由，实现在不集中访问数据的情况下完成多领域视觉-语言的适应性调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）虽然在视觉-语言指令跟随方面表现出色，但将其适应到不同领域通常需要集中式数据访问和高昂的联合训练成本，这在数据分布在私有、领域特定或受权限限制的客户端场景下受到限制。

Method: DistMoE在语言解码器的每一层为公共前馈网络（FFN）配备客户端特定的私有FFN专家；引入公共锚定的专家组合阶段，仅更新路由器和轻量级私有投影适配器，并通过各向同性正则化损失在本地客户端数据和公共数据的混合上进行训练，实现无重复排练的跨客户端组合；推理时通过公共和私有专家之间的模块化路由实现无需领域标签的token级领域组合。

Result: 在多种视觉-语言基准上的实验表明，DistMoE实现了灵活的专家复用、有效的领域适应以及有竞争力的性能，同时在保留客户端特定知识的模块化控制方面表现出色。

Conclusion: DistMoE为分布式环境下的视觉语言指令微调提供了一种有效的MoE解决方案，通过模块化专家设计缓解了独立专家训练带来的表示漂移问题，实现了无需集中数据的领域自适应和专家复用。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown strong multimodal instruction-following ability, but adapting them to diverse visual-language domains typically assumes centralized data access and costly joint training. This is restrictive when data is distributed across private, domain-specific, or permission-limited clients. To this end, we propose DistMoE, a mixture-of-experts (MoE) approach for distributed visual instruction tuning. In each layer of the language decoder it augments the public feedforward network (FFN) with a client-specific private FFN expert, with the goal to acquire domain-specific knowledge. However, independent expert training causes the private FFNs to learn representation of different scale and magnitudes, making merging the experts difficult. To reduce client-specific drift, we introduce a public-anchored expert composition stage that updates only routers and lightweight private projection adapters on a mix of local client data and public data, via an isotropic regularization loss, therefore making it cross-client rehearsal-free composition. During inference, DistMoE performs modular routing over public and private experts, enabling token-wise domain composition without explicit domain labels. Experiments across diverse visual-language benchmarks show that DistMoE enables flexible expert reuse, effective domain adaptation, and competitive performance while preserving modular control over client-specific knowledge. Codes are available at https://github.com/mainaksingha01/DistMoE.

</details>


### [262] [Beyond Hazard Resemblance: Contrastive Event Adjudication for Training-Free Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09908)
*Wenti Yin,Xiang Wang,Huaxin Zhang,Hanqing Wang,Hongbo Shao,Changxin Gao,Nong Sang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CEAVAD，一种无需训练的视频异常检测方法，通过将推理单元从孤立异常概念转变为可证伪的事件假设，利用危险-良性事件对比和解释性裁决机制来确定异常边界，在三个基准数据集上达到无训练范式的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无训练VAD方法虽然利用预训练模型的语义知识和推理能力，但这些能力并不能直接定义异常判定准则——更丰富的异常描述只捕捉了危险的相似性，却无法判定异常性本身。因此需要在推理阶段建立异常决策边界。

Method: CEAVAD首先利用公共安全知识构建危险-良性事件对比，将每个危险机制与通用正常描述和机制特定的良性对应物配对；然后判断目标区间更支持危险解释还是良性竞争解释，产生可修正的对比边界提议；最后在竞争性解释之间进行裁决，判断危险假设是否在视频证据中成立，同时支持时间定位异常检测和证据解释。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的VAD基准数据集上的实验表明，CEAVAD在无训练范式下达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将异常检测从概念分类转变为假设裁决，CEAVAD为无训练视频异常检测提供了一种新的推理范式，在无需目标域标注数据的情况下实现了优越的检测性能。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) aims to identify and temporally localize abnormal events in videos. Supervised methods learn anomaly decision boundaries from target-domain annotations but require substantial in-domain data. Existing training-free methods leverage the rich semantic knowledge and reasoning capabilities of pretrained models to interpret visual content, yet these capabilities do not directly define an anomaly decision criterion: richer anomaly descriptions better capture hazard resemblance without resolving abnormality. To this end, we propose Contrastive Event Adjudication for training-free Video Anomaly Detection (CEAVAD), which shifts the unit of inference from isolated anomaly concepts to falsifiable event hypotheses and establishes an inference-time explanatory boundary through the interaction between competing explanations and video evidence. Specifically, CEAVAD first uses public-safety knowledge to construct hazard-benign event contrasts, pairing each hazard mechanism with a generic normal account and a mechanism-specific benign counterpart. It then determines whether the target interval better supports a hazard explanation or its benign competitor, yielding a revisable contrastive boundary proposal for the target. Finally, CEAVAD adjudicates between the competing explanations to determine whether the hazard hypothesis survives the video evidence, supporting both temporally localized anomaly detection and evidence-grounded explanations. Experiments on three widely used VAD benchmarks demonstrate that CEAVAD achieves state-of-the-art performance under the training-free paradigm.

</details>


### [263] [Learning How the World Evolves: Extrapolative Video World Models via Latent Dynamics Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09926)
*Haodong Li,Shaoteng Liu,Tianyu Wang,Chongjian Ge,Sihui Ji,Jiahan Zhang,Xin Lin,Haolin Lu,Zhe Lin,Manmohan Chandraker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出潜变量动力学推理（LDR），将视频扩散模型的潜变量转移显式建模为运动学积分，仅回归高阶残余动力学，在结构化潜变量上运行，从而在分布外（OOD）场景下显著优于视频扩散基线（误差差距缩小20倍以上），参数减少26倍、速度快143倍，是首个能外推学习动力学到训练分布之外的视频世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型只拟合像素而忽略像素随时间转移的动力学规律，因此能生成视觉上合理的帧，但往往不遵守物理定律。作者希望纯粹从像素中捕捉真实动力学，实现超越训练分布的动力学外推。

Method: LDR将潜变量转移显式建模为运动学积分，低阶动力学通过数值积分处理，模型仅回归驱动滚动的三阶及以上残余项；同时将积分运行在结构化潜变量（而非密集卷积特征）上以获得更好的外推性能。在PhyWorld基准（包含五种任务的受控白盒物理基准）上验证。

Result: 在256²分辨率下，单任务与多任务训练中LDR的分布内与分布外误差差距比视频扩散基线小20倍以上；参数减少26倍，运行速度快143倍。即使面对严重偏移（如仅用红球从左向右运动训练，能正确预测蓝方块从右向左运动）也能泛化。

Conclusion: LDR是首个能将其学习到的动力学外推到训练分布之外的视频世界模型，证明了将显式物理积分引入潜变量转移建模的有效性。

Abstract: The world evolves following its dynamics, i.e., its laws of motion. However, leading video diffusion models largely fit the pixels without modeling how the pixels transit over time. Thus, they render visually plausible frames but may not accurately obey the laws. To capture the dynamics purely from pixels, we introduce Latent Dynamics Reasoning (LDR). LDR casts the latent transition as an explicit kinematic integration, where the lower-order dynamics are integrated numerically and the model regresses only the third- and higher-order residual that drives the rollout. For this integration to extrapolate better, LDR runs it on a structured latent rather than dense convolutional features. Following PhyWorld, we validate LDR on a controlled white-box physics benchmark spanning five tasks (uniform motion, parabola, collision, bouncing, looming), focusing on out-of-distribution scenarios that reveal whether a model has truly learned the underlying dynamics. LDR extrapolates the learned dynamics far better: the gap between its in- and out-of-distribution error is over 20$\times$ smaller than the video diffusion baseline's, under both single- and joint-task training at 256$^2$ resolution, while using 26$\times$ fewer parameters and running 143$\times$ faster. LDR can even generalize under severe shift: for example, trained only on red balls moving left-to-right, it correctly predicts the motion of a blue square moving right-to-left. To our knowledge, this is the first video world model that extrapolates learned dynamics beyond its training distribution. Project page: https://lat-dyn-reason.github.io/

</details>


### [264] [Perception Before Supervision: Self-Contained Visual Distillation from Counterfactual Blind Spots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09931)
*Shravan Venkatraman,Omkar Thawakar,Ritesh Thawkar,Abdelrahman Shaker,Rao Muhammad Anwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CVPD是首个完全自包含的MLLM密集、on-policy、token级视觉自蒸馏框架，通过对比反事实机制识别视觉盲区，将其转化为密集对比监督进行自蒸馏，在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上超越包括依赖外部GPT-4o监督在内的六种baseline，在十二个基准测试上无一回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM自我改进主要依赖基于奖励的方法，仅提供粗粒度的标量反馈；而蒸馏虽然能提供密集的token级监督，但在视觉领域通常依赖外部标注、工具或更强模型构建的特权上下文，缺乏完全自包含的解决方案。

Method: CVPD提出三闸反事实判据，识别模型的视觉盲区——即图像局部区域在放大时能改变并锐化模型答案分布，但移除该区域对全图行为影响不大的区域，这些区域揭示了模型能编码但全图条件下未充分利用的感知信息，并将其转化为密集对比监督进行自蒸馏。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上，CVPD在12个基准测试中优于6种自进化baseline（包括依赖外部GPT-4o监督的方法），无一回归。OCRBench提升+3.60，MMStar细粒度感知提升+3.38，MMStar逻辑推理提升+3.08，同时保持或提高了更广泛的多模态基准性能。

Conclusion: CVPD证明了无需外部标注、工具或更强模型，仅通过模型自身响应识别视觉盲区并转化为密集对比监督，即可实现有效的视觉自蒸馏，为MLLM的完全自包含自我改进提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Self-improvement for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is typically driven by reward-based methods that provide only coarse scalar feedback. Distillation offers a richer alternative through dense token-level supervision, but in the visual domain it usually depends on privileged context constructed using external annotations and tools, or stronger models. We introduce \textbf{CVPD} (Contrastive Counterfactual Visual Process Distillation), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first fully self-contained framework for dense, on-policy, token-level visual self-distillation for MLLMs. CVPD identifies visual blind spots where zooming into a region changes and sharpens the model's answer distribution, while removing the same region leaves the full-image behavior largely unchanged. Such regions reveal perceptual information that the model can encode but fails to consistently utilize under full-image conditioning. We propose a three-gate Counterfactual Criterion that identifies these regions directly from the model's own responses and converts them into dense contrastive supervision for self-distillation. On Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, CVPD outperforms six self-evolving baselines across twelve benchmarks, including methods that rely on external GPT-4o supervision, without a single regression. It achieves gains of $+3.60$ on OCRBench, $+3.38$ on MMStar Fine-Grained Perception, and $+3.08$ on MMStar Logical Reasoning, while maintaining or improving performance on broader multimodal benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [265] [Unified Hallucination Fuzzing for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07525)
*Pengfei Zhou,Jiajun Song,Zhiwei Tang,Yixing Ma,Xiaopeng Peng,Donghui Si,Yuhang Xu,Huiqi Song,Yiyuan Miao,Yichen Qian,Weihua Chen,Wangbo Zhao,Bohan Zhuang,Jiasheng Tang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个系统性的多模态大模型幻觉评估框架，包含细粒度基准数据集UniHall和自适应的多模态模糊测试方法SAMF，通过进化式变异策略探索模型幻觉边界，并发现现有最优MLLMs在模糊测试下性能显著退化，且存在helpfulness-hallucination权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉评估多基于静态基准，存在分类覆盖狭窄和性能饱和问题，无法反映模型在真实动态场景中的鲁棒性，亟需更全面的评估框架。

Method: 提出UniHall细粒度数据集（涵盖对象、指令、知识三个维度），以及SAMF（Self-Adaptive Multimodal Fuzzing）框架，采用进化式突变策略探索模型幻觉边界，并结合多模态oracle集成驱动的结构化指标体系来可靠评估动态输入。

Result: 实验发现最先进的MLLMs在模糊测试下相较常规设置表现出显著性能退化，揭示了推理能力与事实基础之间的脱节；同时发现强化学习对齐会无意中加剧指令遵循任务中的谄媚行为，存在helpfulness-hallucination权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架为多模态幻觉评估提供了更全面、动态的评测方案，揭示了现有MLLMs在实际应用中潜在的幻觉风险及其与对齐策略之间的内在矛盾。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a persistent challenge for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), severely limiting their reliability in high-stakes applications. Existing evaluations, predominantly based on static benchmarks, suffer from narrow taxonomical coverage and rapid performance saturation, failing to reflect model robustness in evolving real-world scenarios. To bridge this gap, we present a systematic evaluation framework integrating a comprehensive benchmark with self-evolving stress testing. First, we introduce UniHall, a fine-grained dataset grounded in a unified taxonomy spanning Object, Instruction, and Knowledge dimensions. Second, to address benchmark saturation, we propose Self-Adaptive Multimodal Fuzzing (SAMF), a self-adaptive framework that employs evolutionary mutation strategies to explore the boundaries of model hallucinations. Crucially, to ensure reliable assessment of dynamic inputs, SAMF incorporates a structured metric suite driven by an ensemble of multi-modal oracles. Our extensive experiments reveal that state-of-the-art MLLMs exhibit significant performance degradation under fuzzing compared to conventional settings, exposing a dissociation between reasoning capabilities and factual grounding. Furthermore, we identify a helpfulness-hallucination trade-off, where reinforcement learning alignment inadvertently exacerbates sycophancy in instruction-following tasks. The framework, code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/LanceZPF/EvalHall.

</details>


### [266] [DocAtlas: Long-Document Understanding as Mutable-State Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07527)
*Hongchen Wei,Yuanzhe Wang,Bei Liu,Yifan Yang,Qi Dai,Kai Qiu,Yunsheng Li,Dongdong Chen,Chong Luo,Zhenzhong Chen,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DocAtlas是一个将长文档理解视为可变状态信息搜寻过程的系统，通过外部文档环境暴露搜索、阅读、笔记和回顾工具，维护分层树和笔记存储，在固定上下文预算下实现自我改进检索、选择性证据访问和主动工作记忆，支持大VLM推理和紧凑VLM的端到端强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强系统通常从静态索引中选择证据，而智能体系统虽支持多轮工具使用但依赖提示词控制的封闭专有模型，缺乏在搜索过程中动态管理文档状态的能力。

Method: DocAtlas将长文档理解建模为可变状态的信息搜寻过程，实例化为可变文档工具箱（mutable document harness）：一个外部环境，决定每一步搜索、阅读、存储、回顾和展示什么文档信息。该工具箱提供搜索、阅读、笔记和回顾工具，维护分层树和笔记存储，并随智能体记录证据而更新，结合自我改进检索、选择性证据访问和主动工作记忆。

Result: 使用GPT-5.4在MMLongBench-Doc上达到71.4%，超过人类专家基准65.8%；使用Qwen3.5-4B VLM在DocAtlas环境中经端到端RL训练达到63.7%，相比直接输入基线54.4%提升显著。

Conclusion: 可变文档工具箱设计能大幅提升紧凑文档智能体的性能，且该框架同时支持大模型的推理时使用和紧凑模型的端到端强化学习训练。

Abstract: Long-document understanding requires models to find and combine evidence across many pages, layouts, tables, figures, and charts. Existing retrieval-augmented systems usually select evidence from a static index before generation, while recent agentic systems add multi-turn tool use but often rely on frozen proprietary backbones whose behavior is set by prompts. We present DocAtlas, a system that treats long-document understanding as a mutable-state information-seeking process. We instantiate DocAtlas as a mutable document harness: an external environment that determines what document information is searched, read, stored, reviewed, and shown to the model at each step. Given a document and question, the harness exposes search, reading, note-taking, and review tools, maintains a hierarchical tree and note store, and updates both as the agent records evidence. DocAtlas combines self-improving retrieval, selective evidence access, and active working memory under a fixed context budget. The same harness supports inference-time use with large VLMs and end-to-end reinforcement learning for compact VLM agents. With GPT-5.4, DocAtlas reaches 71.4\% on MMLongBench-Doc, exceeding the human-expert reference of 65.8\%. A Qwen3.5-4B VLM trained with end-to-end RL in the DocAtlas environment reaches 63.7\%, compared with a 54.4\% direct-input baseline, showing that mutable document-harness design can improve compact document agents by a large margin.

</details>


### [267] [WuYuEval: A Multi-Level Benchmark for Large Language Models in Solid Waste Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07529)
*Yi Zhang,Hongyang Wang,Zheng Hao Leong,Zihao Wu,Kaijun Lin,Zhixing Pan,Qixun Huangfu,Wei Ren,Wenyan Wu,Fangyun Wang,Wenting Yu,Hengyu Lin,Muling Yang,Zongguo Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出WuYuEval，一个多层级固体废物管理（SWM）大语言模型评估基准，包含基础模块（4,590道封闭式选择题）和专家模块（247道开放式情景题），并在33个LLM上进行了系统评测，发现模型在计算、实验设计、城市规划和开放式专家任务上表现薄弱，推理模式的效果取决于基线能力且并非总是正向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估基准过度强调通用知识，缺乏对固体废物管理领域在工程、环境和政策约束下的专业决策能力的评估，因此难以准确衡量LLM在该专业领域的实际胜任力。

Method: 构建WuYuEval两级基准：基础模块含4,590道六种任务类型、八个领域类别的封闭式选择题；专家模块含247道涉及多目标优化、约束权衡和系统设计的情景式开放题。评测采用锚定校准的LLM-as-a-Judge评分结合Elo成对比较。

Result: 在33个LLM上，领先模型基础模块准确率达94.64%，但平均准确率从简单题的84.14%降至难题的42.50%；计算、实验设计、城市规划和开放专家任务是主要短板。推理模式能改善多数匹配模型对，但效果依赖基线能力且并非一致正向。

Conclusion: WuYuEval既是评估资源，也为开发具有专业推理链和显式约束控制的SWM领域基础模型提供了实证基础。可见的推理只有锚定在单位、假设和工程约束上才有效，否则可能偏离决定性答案边界。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as technical assistants, but their competence in solid waste management (SWM) remains difficult to assess because existing benchmarks emphasize general knowledge rather than professional decisions under engineering, environmental, and policy constraints. We introduce WuYuEval, a multi-level benchmark for evaluating LLMs in SWM across foundational knowledge, domain reasoning, and expert decision-making. After quality auditing, WuYuEval contains a Foundation Module with 4,590 closed-ended multiple-choice questions across six task types and eight domain categories, together with an Expert Module with 247 scenario-based open-ended questions involving multi-objective optimization, constraint trade-offs, and system design. For expert tasks, we combine anchor-calibrated LLM-as-a-Judge scoring with Elo-based pairwise comparison. Across 33 LLMs, performance varied widely. The leading model reached 94.64\% accuracy on the Foundation Module, but average accuracy still fell from 84.14\% on easy questions to 42.50\% on hard questions, with lower performance concentrated in calculation, experimental design, urban planning, and open-ended expert tasks. Reasoning-oriented Thinking modes improve most matched model pairs after auditing, but the gains depend on baseline capability and are not uniformly positive. These results suggest that visible deliberation helps only when it remains anchored to units, assumptions, and engineering constraints; otherwise, it may drift from decisive answer boundaries. WuYuEval therefore provides both an evaluation resource and an empirical basis for developing SWM-oriented foundation models with professional reasoning chains and explicit constraint control.

</details>


### [268] [Search-G1: Grounded Search Agents via Representation-Based Intrinsic Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07531)
*Cheng Ruoxi,Ma Haoxuan,Zhang Hongyi,Zhang Junming,Duan Ranjie,Xia Qiaolin,Wang Hao,Lu Yu,Shi Haibo,Ma Xingjun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Search-G1，一种基于表示的内部奖励框架，通过两个干预校准的读数（提示状态读数和答案承诺读数）分别衡量封闭式知识充分性和证据依赖度，从而在无需过程标注或LLM评判器的情况下，为搜索增强语言代理提供细粒度、廉价且与策略共同演化的奖励信号，改善grounding与搜索成本之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的搜索增强语言代理外部奖励要么太稀疏（仅结果监督），要么需要昂贵的标注或推理（过程注释和LLM评判器）；内部奖励虽然廉价且细粒度，但主要反映模型置信度而非证据grounding。因此需要一个既廉价又能准确衡量检索必要性和证据依赖度的奖励机制。

Method: 提出Search-G1框架，包含两个干预校准的表示读数：(1) 提示状态读数用于预测封闭式知识是否充分，其补集定义策略相对的检索必要性；(2) 答案承诺读数评估答案对证据删除的敏感性，估算证据依赖度。两者共同为正确的搜索轨迹提供额外奖励、在封闭式知识充足时偏好直接回答，并惩罚重复搜索。奖励无需过程标注或LLM评判器，且通过周期性地基于最新策略checkpoint重新拟合读数，使奖励与策略共同演化。

Result: 在多个基于搜索的问答基准和两种模型规模上，Search-G1改善了grounding与搜索成本之间的权衡，在保持竞争力的任务准确率的同时产生更短的响应侧轨迹。

Conclusion: Search-G1证明了基于表示的内部奖励可以有效地替代昂贵的外部奖励信号，实现既细粒度又低成本的奖励设计，并在实际搜索问答任务中取得了更好的grounding-成本平衡。

Abstract: Search-augmented language agents should retrieve external information only when necessary and ground their answers in retrieved evidence. Existing external rewards provide either sparse outcome supervision or richer feedback from process annotations and LLM judges. Outcome rewards scale readily but cannot distinguish grounded retrieval from redundant search, whereas richer signals require costly annotation or inference during training. Internal rewards based on policy-side signals such as entropy, likelihood, or information gain are graded and inexpensive to evaluate, yet mainly reflect model confidence rather than evidence grounding. We propose Search-G1, a representation-based intrinsic reward framework that measures the operational grounding of an agent's answers through two intervention-calibrated readouts. A prompt-state readout predicts closed-book sufficiency, whose complement defines policy-relative retrieval necessity; an answer-commit readout estimates evidence reliance from answer-stage sensitivity to evidence deletion. Together, they provide additional credit to correct searched trajectories when retrieval is estimated necessary and the answer is evidence-sensitive, favor correct direct answers when closed-book knowledge suffices, and penalize repeated search. After calibration, reward scoring requires neither process annotations nor LLM-as-judge inference during policy optimization. Because reinforcement learning changes policy representations, Search-G1 periodically refits both readouts on trajectories from the latest checkpoint, allowing the reward to co-evolve with the policy. Experiments across multiple search-based question-answering benchmarks and two model scales show that Search-G1 improves the grounding--search-cost trade-off, producing shorter response-side trajectories at competitive task accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/Rosy0912/Search-G1.

</details>


### [269] [Scaling Inherently Interpretable Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07594)
*Guide Labs Team,Andreas Madsen,Aya Abdelsalam Ismail,Giang Nguyen,Isaac Plant,Muawiz Chaudhary,Nathaniel Monson,Saqib Azim,Zhichen Guo,Julius Adebayo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"可解释性是能力的负担"这一传统观点，提出将可解释性作为训练目标的一部分进行优化。在三个数量级的计算规模下，可解释性与能力同步提升而非相互制约。作者构建了Steerling-8B模型，一个带因果注意力掩码的扩散语言模型，支持对生成令牌进行归因（输入令牌、概念、训练数据），实现无需重训练的概念引导干预，且在计算量仅10-50%的情况下与开放同行模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可解释性方法是在模型训练完成后事后进行逆向工程，其可靠性难以验证。作者希望将可解释性嵌入训练流程，使其成为训练目标的一部分，而非事后附加的"税"，从而从根本上解决可解释方法与模型能力脱节的问题。

Method: 将可解释性作为训练管道中的约束条件，与语言建模目标联合优化。在自回归和扩散两类语言模型上，跨三个数量级的计算规模进行实验验证。最终实例化为Steerling-8B，一个带因果注意力掩码的扩散语言模型，该模型能对输出令牌进行输入令牌、人类可理解概念及训练数据的多重归因。

Result: 可解释性与模型能力随规模同步提升，模型表示变得更加解耦并与人类可理解概念对齐。Steerling-8B在训练计算量仅为开放同行模型的1/16至1/2的情况下仍保持竞争力，展示了将可解释性设计进训练过程的全新扩展范式。

Conclusion: 可解释性可以通过设计融入训练而非事后附加，且随规模增大而增强，不代表能力的损失。这开启了一种不同的扩展范式：将可解释性作为训练目标的一部分，可以实现诊断（归因）、检索（训练数据）和纠正（概念引导）的闭环干预，无需重训练。

Abstract: Interpretability is often treated as a tax on capability: language models are trained as opaque systems, then explained after the fact, with methods whose reliability is difficult to establish. In this work, we challenge this premise. Rather than reverse-engineering a model, we make interpretability a constraint of the training pipeline, optimized alongside the language modeling objective. Across three orders of magnitude of compute, on both autoregressive and diffusion language models, interpretability scales with capability rather than against it. Surprisingly, model representations become more disentangled and aligned with human-understandable concepts with scale.
  We instantiate the training-time recipe with Steerling-8B, a diffusion language model with a causal attention mask. For any group of generated tokens, Steerling-8B attributes the output to relevant input tokens, human-understandable concepts, and training data. This enables closed-loop intervention: diagnose an output through its concept or feature attribution, retrieve similar training data, and correct the behavior through concept steering without retraining. Steerling-8B remains competitive with open peer models trained on substantially 2-16x more compute, suggesting a different scaling paradigm: interpretability can be designed into training, and it improves with scale.

</details>


### [270] [Embedding Initialization for Unseen Low-resource Languages in Multilingual NMT: A Case Study on Limbum-English Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07629)
*Samiratu Ntohsi,Neza David Tuyishimire,Anesu Kafesu,Marvin Ogore,Samuel Oluwajunwonlo Babalola,Oche Ankeli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Multilingual neural machine translation models such as NLLB-200 cover 200 languages but leave thousands unsupported, including most Grassfields Bantu languages of Cameroon. When fine-tuning these models for an unseen language, practitioners must choose a proxy language token, yet no principled method exists for this selection. We implemented an embedding initialization strategy where a language token is the average of embeddings from multiple typologically related languages already in the mod el. We evaluate this approach on Limbum-to-English translation using a parallel corpus of 8,837 sentence pairs from New Testament text and a bilingual dictionary. We compare models: NLLB-200 zero-shot (chrF2++ = 12.5), a Transformer trained from scratch (chrF2++ = 14.5), NLLB-200 fine-tuned with a Swahili proxy token (chrF2++ = 47.3), and NLLB-200 with our averaged embedding initialization (chrF2++ = 46.7). We find that the multi-language initialization achieves performance comparable to the best single-language proxy. Both NLLB-200 variants improve over the from-scratch baseline by over 32 chrF2++ points. These results show that multilingual transfer is the dominant factor in extremely low-resource Bantu translation while eliminating the need for heuristic proxy selection. However, all systems fail to preserve tonal diacritics, highlighting an open challenge. We make our dataset and code available to support further research.

</details>


### [271] [SurveyReview: A Reviewer-Aligned Benchmark for Survey Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07641)
*Yuheng Zhang,Yuanchun Wang,Fanjin Zhang,Ruyu Zhao,Juanzi Li,Jie Tang,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SurveyReview基准数据集与SurveyAlign评估器，通过收集675篇论文的1630份评审报告并转化为四维评分标注，显著提升了LLM评审与人类评审员的对齐程度（MSE从2.28降至1.38）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM自动化撰写综述的规模化发展，可靠评估成为瓶颈，但现有LLM-as-a-judge方法缺乏与人类评审员的系统对齐，也缺少量化对齐程度的框架。

Method: 收集并标注675篇论文的1,630份评审报告，将自由文本评审转化为四维度评分（可读性、批判性、全面性、结构）并附带支持性依据；发布标准化训练/测试划分和评估协议；开发SurveyAlign基线评估器，基于Qwen3-32B进行LoRA微调并辅以外部知识增强。

Result: SurveyAlign在测试集上显著优于基于GPT-5.2的提示式评判，四个维度的平均MSE从2.28降至1.38，MAE从1.15降至0.69。

Conclusion: 建立了首个多维评审者对齐的综述评审数据集及可复现评估框架，并提供了与人类评审员高度对齐的强基线评估器，为后续研究提供竞争性参考。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of large language models has transformed survey writing from a months-long manual effort into an automated process. As generation scales, reliable evaluation becomes the bottleneck, and LLMs are increasingly used as survey evaluators. However, existing approaches largely rely on off-the-shelf LLM-as-a-judge methods without systematic alignment to human reviewers, and there remains a lack of systematic frameworks for quantifying alignment with human reviewers. To address this gap, we propose SurveyReview, a reviewer-aligned, multi-dimensional benchmark and dataset for survey evaluation. We collect and annotate 675 survey papers with 1,630 review reports. We structure authentic peer-review reports by converting free-form comments into four-dimensional scores (Readability, Criticalness, Comprehensiveness, Structure) paired with supporting rationales. We further release standardized train/test splits and an evaluation protocol to measure alignment between automatic evaluators and human reviewers. To validate the benchmark, we develop SurveyAlign, a strong baseline evaluator by fine-tuning Qwen3-32B with LoRA on our annotated data, augmented with external knowledge for knowledge-intensive dimensions. On the test set, SurveyAlign substantially improves reviewer alignment over prompt-based judging with GPT-5.2, reducing average MSE from 2.28 to 1.38 and MAE from 1.15 to 0.69 across all four dimensions. Our contributions are twofold: (1) we establish the first multi-dimensional, reviewer-aligned dataset with a reproducible evaluation framework for survey reviewing; (2) we develop a strong baseline evaluator that substantially improves alignment with human reviewers, providing a competitive reference for future research. Our code and data are available at https://surveyreview.github.io

</details>


### [272] [Evaluating Dedicated Monolingual and Joint Multilingual Causal Models for Dravidian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07727)
*Venkata Naga Sai Vishnu Rohit Pulipaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文训练了五个GPT-2架构模型（四个达罗毗荼语言单语模型+一个多语言共享模型），并与mGPT对比，发现单语模型在情感分类和命名实体识别上表现更好，分词器效率也更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 达罗毗荼语言（泰米尔语、泰卢固语、卡纳达语和马拉雅拉姆语）在多语言语言模型训练数据中占比很小，因此不清楚这些模型对这些语言的实际能力保留了多少。

Method: 从零训练五个GPT-2架构模型：四个单语模型（每种语言配32K词表子词分词器）和一个多语言模型（四种语言共享64K词表子词分词器）。所有模型在清洗后的CC-100、Wikipedia和Samanantar数据上训练，并与mGPT在困惑度、每字节比特数、分词器效率和微调结果上进行比较。

Result: 单语模型在情感分类和命名实体识别任务上优于mGPT，且分词器在所有测试语言上比共享多语言模型更高效。

Conclusion: 对于达罗毗荼语言，训练单语GPT-2模型比依赖共享多语言模型更有效，分词器效率和下游任务性能均更优。

Abstract: Dravidian languages, mainly Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam make up only a small part of the data used to train multilingual language models, so it's not clear how much per-language ability these models actually keep. I have trained five GPT-2 architecture models from scratch to compare four monolingual models (one each for Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam, each with its own 32K-vocabulary subword tokenizer) against one multilingual model sharing a 64K-vocabulary subword tokenizer across all four languages. All the 5 models are trained on cleaned CC-100, Wikipedia, and Samanantar data.
